   3: import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceFuturesMarketDataClient;
   4: import com.crypto.client.binance.dto.BinanceFundingRate;
   5: import com.crypto.client.binance.dto.BinanceOpenInterestPoint;
   6: import com.crypto.config.DerivativesPositioningProperties;
   7: import com.crypto.domain.Candle;
   8: import com.crypto.domain.DerivativesPositioningStatus;
   9: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
  10: import com.crypto.dto.DerivativesPositioningResult;
  11: import com.crypto.repository.CandleRepository;
  12: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
  13: import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
  14: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  15: import org.springframework.web.client.RestClientResponseException;
  16: 
  17: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  18: import java.math.RoundingMode;
  19: import java.time.Instant;
  20: import java.util.ArrayList;
  21: import java.util.Comparator;
  22: import java.util.List;
  23: 
  24: @Service
  25: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  26: public class DerivativesPositioningService {
  27:     private static final BigDecimal ONE_HUNDRED = new BigDecimal("100");
  28: 
  29:     private final BinanceFuturesMarketDataClient client;
  30:     private final DerivativesPositioningProperties properties;
  31:     private final CandleRepository candleRepository;
  32: 
  33:     /**
  34:      * Historical replay must never call the live Binance futures endpoints.
  35:      * Derivatives history is not persisted by this application, so the only
  36:      * truthful replay result is UNAVAILABLE for that historical timestamp.
  37:      */
  38:     public DerivativesPositioningResult evaluateHistorical(String symbol, String interval, SignalDecision decision,
  39:                                                             boolean entryAllowed, Instant evaluatedAt) {
  40:         Instant time = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
  41:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
  42:             return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  43:                     null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
  44:                     "Derivatives positioning is disabled.", time);
  45:         }
  46:         return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.UNAVAILABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  47:                 null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
  48:                 "Historical derivatives positioning was not persisted; live Binance data is intentionally not used during replay.",
  49:                 time);
  50:     }
  51: 
  52:     public DerivativesPositioningResult evaluate(String symbol, String interval, SignalDecision decision,
  53:                                                   boolean entryAllowed, Instant evaluatedAt) {
  54:         Instant now = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
  55:         if (!properties.enabled()) return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  56:                 null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
  57:                 "Derivatives positioning is disabled.", now);
  58:         try {
  59:             List<BinanceFundingRate> funding = client.fundingHistory(symbol, properties.fundingHistoryLimit());
  60:             List<BinanceOpenInterestPoint> oi = client.openInterestHistory(symbol, period(interval), properties.openInterestHistoryLimit());
  61:             if (funding.isEmpty() || oi.size() < 2) {
  62:                 return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.LEARNING, decision, entryAllowed,
  63:                         latestFunding(funding), percentile(funding), latestOi(oi), latestOiValue(oi), null,
  64:                         priceChange(symbol, interval), funding.size(), period(interval), -5,
  65:                         "Funding or open-interest history is still insufficient; no veto was applied.", now);
  66:             }
  67:             BigDecimal fundingRate = latestFunding(funding);
  68:             BigDecimal fundingPercentile = percentile(funding);
  69:             BigDecimal oiChange = percentChange(oi.get(oi.size() - 2).sumOpenInterest(), oi.get(oi.size() - 1).sumOpenInterest());
  70:             BigDecimal priceChange = priceChange(symbol, interval);
  71:             DerivativesPositioningStatus status = classify(fundingRate, oiChange, priceChange);
  72:             SignalDecision finalDecision = decision;
  73:             boolean allowed = entryAllowed;
  74:             int confidence = confidence(status);
  75:             if (isBullish(decision) && status == DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONGS_CROWDED
  76:                     && properties.vetoExtremeCrowding()
  77:                     && oiChange != null && oiChange.abs().compareTo(properties.strongOpenInterestChangeThreshold()) >= 0) {
  78:                 finalDecision = SignalDecision.WATCH;
  79:                 allowed = false;
  80:             }
  81:             if (isBearish(decision) && status == DerivativesPositioningStatus.SHORTS_CROWDED
  82:                     && properties.vetoExtremeCrowding()
  83:                     && oiChange != null && oiChange.abs().compareTo(properties.strongOpenInterestChangeThreshold()) >= 0) {
  84:                 finalDecision = SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
  85:                 allowed = false;
  86:             }
  87:             String explanation = explanation(status, fundingRate, fundingPercentile, oiChange, priceChange, allowed);
  88:             BinanceOpenInterestPoint latest = oi.get(oi.size() - 1);
  89:             return result(status, finalDecision, allowed, fundingRate, fundingPercentile,
  90:                     latest.sumOpenInterest(), latest.sumOpenInterestValue(), oiChange, priceChange,
  91:                     funding.size(), period(interval), confidence, explanation, now);
  92:         } catch (RestClientResponseException ex) {
  93:             if (ex.getStatusCode().value() == 400) {
  94:                 return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.NOT_APPLICABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  95:                         null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), 0,
  96:                         "No matching Binance USD-M perpetual contract is available for " + symbol + ".", now);
  97:             }
  98:             return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.UNAVAILABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
  99:                     null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), -10,
 100:                     "Binance futures positioning data is unavailable: " + ex.getMessage(), now);
 101:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 102:             return result(DerivativesPositioningStatus.UNAVAILABLE, decision, entryAllowed,
 103:                     null, null, null, null, null, null, 0, period(interval), -10,
 104:                     "Binance futures positioning data is unavailable: " + ex.getMessage(), now);
 105:         }
 106:     }
 107: 
 108:     private DerivativesPositioningStatus classify(BigDecimal funding, BigDecimal oiChange, BigDecimal priceChange) {
 109:         if (funding != null && funding.compareTo(properties.extremeFundingRate()) >= 0) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONGS_CROWDED;
 110:         if (funding != null && funding.compareTo(properties.extremeFundingRate().negate()) <= 0) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.SHORTS_CROWDED;
 111:         if (oiChange == null || priceChange == null) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.BALANCED;
 112:         boolean oiRising = oiChange.compareTo(properties.openInterestChangeThreshold()) >= 0;
 113:         boolean oiFalling = oiChange.compareTo(properties.openInterestChangeThreshold().negate()) <= 0;
 114:         if (priceChange.signum() > 0 && oiRising) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.FRESH_LONG_BUILDUP;
 115:         if (priceChange.signum() < 0 && oiRising) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.FRESH_SHORT_BUILDUP;
 116:         if (priceChange.signum() > 0 && oiFalling) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.SHORT_COVERING;
 117:         if (priceChange.signum() < 0 && oiFalling) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.LONG_LIQUIDATION;
 118:         if (funding != null && funding.compareTo(properties.moderateFundingRate()) >= 0) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.HEALTHY_BULLISH;
 119:         if (funding != null && funding.compareTo(properties.moderateFundingRate().negate()) <= 0) return DerivativesPositioningStatus.HEALTHY_BEARISH;
 120:         return oiChange.abs().compareTo(properties.openInterestChangeThreshold()) < 0
 121:                 ? DerivativesPositioningStatus.LOW_CONVICTION : DerivativesPositioningStatus.BALANCED;
 122:     }
 123: 
 124:     private int confidence(DerivativesPositioningStatus status) {
 125:         return switch (status) {
 126:             case FRESH_LONG_BUILDUP, FRESH_SHORT_BUILDUP -> 8;
 127:             case HEALTHY_BULLISH, HEALTHY_BEARISH, BALANCED -> 3;
 128:             case SHORT_COVERING, LONG_LIQUIDATION, LOW_CONVICTION -> -5;
 129:             case LONGS_CROWDED, SHORTS_CROWDED -> -15;
 130:             case LEARNING -> -5;
 131:             case UNAVAILABLE -> -10;
 132:             case NOT_APPLICABLE -> 0;
 133:         };
 134:     }
 135: 
 136:     private String explanation(DerivativesPositioningStatus status, BigDecimal funding, BigDecimal percentile,
 137:                                BigDecimal oiChange, BigDecimal priceChange, boolean allowed) {
 138:         return "Positioning " + status + "; funding=" + value(funding) + ", percentile=" + value(percentile)
 139:                 + "%, OI change=" + value(oiChange) + "%, price change=" + value(priceChange)
 140:                 + "%. Entry " + (allowed ? "remains allowed" : "was blocked") + ".";
 141:     }
 142: 
 143:     private BigDecimal priceChange(String symbol, String interval) {
 144:         List<Candle> candles = new ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandles(symbol, interval, PageRequest.of(0, 2)));
 145:         if (candles.size() < 2) return null;
 146:         candles.sort(Comparator.comparing(Candle::getOpenTime));
 147:         return percentChange(candles.get(0).getClosePrice(), candles.get(1).getClosePrice());
 148:     }
 149: 
 150:     private BigDecimal latestFunding(List<BinanceFundingRate> rates) {
 151:         return rates.isEmpty() ? null : rates.get(rates.size() - 1).fundingRate();
 152:     }
 153:     private BigDecimal latestOi(List<BinanceOpenInterestPoint> points) { return points.isEmpty() ? null : points.get(points.size()-1).sumOpenInterest(); }
 154:     private BigDecimal latestOiValue(List<BinanceOpenInterestPoint> points) { return points.isEmpty() ? null : points.get(points.size()-1).sumOpenInterestValue(); }
 155: 
 156:     private BigDecimal percentile(List<BinanceFundingRate> rates) {
 157:         if (rates.size() < properties.minimumFundingSamples()) return null;
 158:         BigDecimal latest = latestFunding(rates);
 159:         long lessOrEqual = rates.stream().map(BinanceFundingRate::fundingRate).filter(v -> v.compareTo(latest) <= 0).count();
 160:         return BigDecimal.valueOf(lessOrEqual).multiply(ONE_HUNDRED)
 161:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(rates.size()), 2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 162:     }
 163: 
 164:     private BigDecimal percentChange(BigDecimal before, BigDecimal after) {
 165:         if (before == null || after == null || before.signum() == 0) return null;
 166:         return after.subtract(before).divide(before.abs(), 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(ONE_HUNDRED);
 167:     }
 168: 
 169:     private String period(String interval) {
 170:         return switch (interval) {
 171:             case "1m", "5m" -> "5m";
 172:             case "15m" -> "15m";
 173:             case "30m" -> "30m";
 174:             case "1h" -> "1h";
 175:             case "2h" -> "2h";
 176:             case "4h" -> "4h";
 177:             case "6h" -> "6h";
 178:             case "12h" -> "12h";
 179:             case "1d" -> "1d";
 180:             default -> "5m";
 181:         };
 182:     }
 183: 
 184:     private boolean isBullish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.BUY || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY; }
 185:     private boolean isBearish(SignalDecision d) { return d == SignalDecision.SELL || d == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL; }
 186:     private String value(BigDecimal v) { return v == null ? "n/a" : v.stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString(); }
 187: 
 188:     private DerivativesPositioningResult result(DerivativesPositioningStatus status, SignalDecision decision,
 189:             boolean allowed, BigDecimal funding, BigDecimal percentile, BigDecimal oi, BigDecimal oiValue,
 190:             BigDecimal oiChange, BigDecimal priceChange, int samples, String period, int confidence,
 191:             String explanation, Instant time) {
 192:         return new DerivativesPositioningResult(status, decision, allowed, funding, percentile, oi, oiValue,
 193:                 oiChange, priceChange, samples, period, confidence, explanation, time);
 194:     }
 195: }

### src/main/java/com/crypto/service/SentimentService.java
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.config.SentimentProperties;
   4: import com.crypto.domain.SentimentSignal;
   5: import com.crypto.dto.ProviderSentiment;
   6: import com.crypto.dto.SentimentOverview;
   7: import com.crypto.dto.SentimentRequest;
   8: import com.crypto.dto.SentimentTextRequest;
   9: import com.crypto.repository.SentimentSignalRepository;
  10: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
  11: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  12: import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
  13: 
  14: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  15: import java.math.MathContext;
  16: import java.time.Duration;
  17: import java.time.Instant;
  18: import java.util.ArrayList;
  19: import java.util.Comparator;
  20: import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
  21: import java.util.List;
  22: import java.util.Map;
  23: 
  24: @Service
  25: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  26: public class SentimentService {
  27: 
  28:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  29: 
  30:     private final SentimentSignalRepository repository;
  31:     private final SentimentTextAnalyzer textAnalyzer;
  32:     private final SentimentProperties properties;
  33:     private final SentimentProviderConfigService providerConfigService;
  34: 
  35:     @Transactional
  36:     public SentimentSignal save(SentimentRequest request) {
  37:         validateRange(request.score(), "score");
  38:         validateConfidence(request.confidence());
  39:         return persist(request.symbol(), request.score(), request.confidence(), request.source(), request.summary());
  40:     }
  41: 
  42:     @Transactional
  43:     public SentimentSignal analyzeAndSave(SentimentTextRequest request) {
  44:         SentimentTextAnalyzer.Result result = textAnalyzer.analyze(request.text());
  45:         String summary = "Text sentiment: positive=" + result.positiveMatches()
  46:                 + ", negative=" + result.negativeMatches()
  47:                 + ". " + abbreviate(request.text(), 700);
  48:         return persist(request.symbol(), result.score(), result.confidence(), request.source(), summary);
  49:     }
  50: 
  51:     @Transactional
  52:     public SentimentSignal analyzeAndSave(
  53:             String symbol,
  54:             String provider,
  55:             String text,
  56:             Instant observedAt
  57:     ) {
  58:         SentimentTextAnalyzer.Result result = textAnalyzer.analyze(text);
  59:         String normalizedSymbol = normalizeSymbol(symbol);
  60:         String normalizedProvider = SentimentProviderName.normalize(provider);
  61:         String normalizedSummary = abbreviate(text, 1000);
  62:         Instant timestamp = observedAt == null ? Instant.now() : observedAt;
  63:         if (repository.existsBySymbolAndSourceAndObservedAtAndSummary(
  64:                 normalizedSymbol, normalizedProvider, timestamp, normalizedSummary)) {
  65:             return null;
  66:         }
  67:         return repository.save(SentimentSignal.builder()
  68:                 .symbol(normalizedSymbol)
  69:                 .score(result.score())
  70:                 .confidence(result.confidence())
  71:                 .source(normalizedProvider)
  72:                 .summary(normalizedSummary)
  73:                 .observedAt(timestamp)
  74:                 .build());
  75:     }
  76: 
  77:     @Transactional
  78:     public SentimentSignal saveProviderScore(
  79:             String symbol,
  80:             String provider,
  81:             BigDecimal score,
  82:             BigDecimal confidence,
  83:             String summary,
  84:             Instant observedAt
  85:     ) {
  86:         validateRange(score, "score");
  87:         validateConfidence(confidence);
  88:         String normalizedSymbol = normalizeSymbol(symbol);
  89:         String normalizedProvider = SentimentProviderName.normalize(provider);
  90:         String normalizedSummary = abbreviate(summary == null ? "" : summary, 1000);
  91:         Instant timestamp = observedAt == null ? Instant.now() : observedAt;
  92:         if (repository.existsBySymbolAndSourceAndObservedAtAndSummary(
  93:                 normalizedSymbol, normalizedProvider, timestamp, normalizedSummary)) {
  94:             return null;
  95:         }
  96:         return repository.save(SentimentSignal.builder()
  97:                 .symbol(normalizedSymbol)
  98:                 .score(score)
  99:                 .confidence(confidence)
 100:                 .source(normalizedProvider)
 101:                 .summary(normalizedSummary)
 102:                 .observedAt(timestamp)
 103:                 .build());
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 107:     public SentimentOverview overview(String symbol) {
 108:         return overviewAsOf(symbol, Instant.now());
 109:     }
 110: 
 111:     /**
 112:      * Historical/as-of sentiment view. No sentiment row newer than evaluatedAt
 113:      * is visible to the caller. This keeps Administration replay deterministic
 114:      * and prevents current/future sentiment from leaking into historical runs.
 115:      */
 116:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 117:     public SentimentOverview overviewAsOf(String symbol, Instant evaluatedAt) {
 118:         String normalized = normalizeSymbol(symbol);
 119:         Instant reference = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
 120:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
 121:             return new SentimentOverview(
 122:                     normalized,
 123:                     BigDecimal.ZERO,
 124:                     "DISABLED",
 125:                     0,
 126:                     List.of(),
 127:                     List.of()
 128:             );
 129:         }
 130:         List<SentimentSignal> recent = repository
 131:                 .findTop20BySymbolAndObservedAtLessThanEqualOrderByObservedAtDesc(normalized, reference);
 132:         Aggregation aggregation = aggregate(normalized, reference);
 133:         return new SentimentOverview(
 134:                 normalized,
 135:                 aggregation.score(),
 136:                 label(aggregation.score()),
 137:                 aggregation.sampleCount(),
 138:                 aggregation.providers(),
 139:                 recent
 140:         );
 141:     }
 142: 
 143:     /**
 144:      * Score consumed by AnalysisService. When sentiment is disabled, it safely
 145:      * returns zero and does not query or aggregate sentiment rows.
 146:      */
 147:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 148:     public BigDecimal currentScore(String symbol) {
 149:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
 150:             return BigDecimal.ZERO;
 151:         }
 152:         return aggregate(normalizeSymbol(symbol), Instant.now()).score();
 153:     }
 154: 
 155:     /**
 156:      * Kept for compatibility with existing callers. Prefer currentScore().
 157:      */
 158:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 159:     public BigDecimal weightedScore(String symbol) {
 160:         return currentScore(symbol);
 161:     }
 162: 
 163:     public boolean isEnabled() {
 164:         return properties.enabled();
 165:     }
 166: 
 167:     private Aggregation aggregate(String symbol, Instant reference) {
 168:         Instant now = reference == null ? Instant.now() : reference;
 169:         Instant after = now.minus(properties.activeWindow());
 170:         List<SentimentSignal> signals = repository
 171:                 .findBySymbolAndObservedAtAfterAndObservedAtLessThanEqualOrderByObservedAtDesc(
 172:                         symbol, after, now);
 173: 
 174:         Map<String, List<SentimentSignal>> byProvider = new LinkedHashMap<>();
 175:         for (SentimentSignal signal : signals) {
 176:             String provider = SentimentProviderName.normalize(signal.getSource());
 177:             byProvider.computeIfAbsent(provider, ignored -> new ArrayList<>()).add(signal);
 178:         }
 179: 
 180:         for (com.crypto.domain.SentimentProviderConfig configured : providerConfigService.findAll()) {
 181:             byProvider.computeIfAbsent(configured.getProviderCode(), ignored -> new ArrayList<>());
 182:         }
 183: 
 184:         List<ProviderSentiment> breakdown = new ArrayList<>();
 185:         BigDecimal total = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 186:         BigDecimal denominator = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 187: 
 188:         for (Map.Entry<String, List<SentimentSignal>> entry : byProvider.entrySet()) {
 189:             String providerName = entry.getKey();
 190:             com.crypto.domain.SentimentProviderConfig providerConfig = providerConfigService.require(providerName);
 191:             ProviderAggregate provider = aggregateProvider(entry.getValue(), now);
 192:             BigDecimal configuredWeight = providerConfig.getWeight();
 193:             BigDecimal effectiveWeight = provider.confidence().multiply(configuredWeight, MC);
 194: 
 195:             boolean historicalReference = now.isBefore(Instant.now().minusSeconds(1));
 196:             boolean healthyEnough = historicalReference
 197:                     ? provider.sampleCount() > 0
 198:                     : providerConfigService.contributes(providerConfig, now);
 199:             if (properties.enabled() && providerConfig.isEnabled() && healthyEnough
 200:                     && provider.sampleCount() > 0 && effectiveWeight.signum() > 0) {
 201:                 total = total.add(provider.score().multiply(effectiveWeight, MC), MC);
 202:                 denominator = denominator.add(effectiveWeight, MC);
 203:             }
 204: 
 205:             breakdown.add(new ProviderSentiment(
 206:                     providerName,
 207:                     properties.enabled() && providerConfig.isEnabled(),
 208:                     configuredWeight,
 209:                     provider.score(),
 210:                     provider.confidence(),
 211:                     properties.enabled() && providerConfig.isEnabled() && healthyEnough
 212:                             ? effectiveWeight : BigDecimal.ZERO,
 213:                     provider.sampleCount(),
 214:                     provider.latestObservedAt()
 215:             ));
 216:         }
 217: 
 218:         breakdown.sort(Comparator
 219:                 .comparing(ProviderSentiment::effectiveWeight).reversed()
 220:                 .thenComparing(ProviderSentiment::provider));
 221: 
 222:         BigDecimal score = denominator.signum() == 0
 223:                 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
 224:                 : total.divide(denominator, MC);
 225:         return new Aggregation(score, signals.size(), breakdown);
 226:     }
 227: 
 228:     private ProviderAggregate aggregateProvider(List<SentimentSignal> signals, Instant now) {
 229:         if (signals.isEmpty()) {
 230:             return new ProviderAggregate(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO, 0, null);
 231:         }
 232: 
 233:         BigDecimal weightedTotal = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 234:         BigDecimal totalSampleWeight = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 235:         BigDecimal confidenceTotal = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 236:         Instant latest = null;
 237: 
 238:         for (SentimentSignal signal : signals) {
 239:             long ageMinutes = Math.max(0, Duration.between(signal.getObservedAt(), now).toMinutes());
 240:             BigDecimal timeWeight = BigDecimal.ONE.divide(
 241:                     BigDecimal.ONE.add(BigDecimal.valueOf(ageMinutes / 240.0), MC), MC);
 242:             BigDecimal sampleWeight = signal.getConfidence().multiply(timeWeight, MC);
 243: 
 244:             weightedTotal = weightedTotal.add(signal.getScore().multiply(sampleWeight, MC), MC);
 245:             totalSampleWeight = totalSampleWeight.add(sampleWeight, MC);
 246:             confidenceTotal = confidenceTotal.add(signal.getConfidence().multiply(timeWeight, MC), MC);
 247:             if (latest == null || signal.getObservedAt().isAfter(latest)) latest = signal.getObservedAt();
 248:         }
 249: 
 250:         BigDecimal score = totalSampleWeight.signum() == 0
 251:                 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
 252:                 : weightedTotal.divide(totalSampleWeight, MC);
 253:         BigDecimal confidence = confidenceTotal
 254:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(signals.size()), MC)
 255:                 .min(BigDecimal.ONE);
 256:         return new ProviderAggregate(score, confidence, signals.size(), latest);
 257:     }
 258: 
 259:     public String label(BigDecimal score) {
 260:         if (score.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.35)) >= 0) return "BULLISH";
 261:         if (score.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.10)) >= 0) return "SLIGHTLY_BULLISH";
 262:         if (score.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(-0.10)) > 0) return "NEUTRAL";
 263:         if (score.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(-0.35)) > 0) return "SLIGHTLY_BEARISH";
 264:         return "BEARISH";
 265:     }
 266: 
 267:     private SentimentSignal persist(String symbol, BigDecimal score, BigDecimal confidence, String source, String summary) {
 268:         return repository.save(SentimentSignal.builder()
 269:                 .symbol(normalizeSymbol(symbol))
 270:                 .score(score)
 271:                 .confidence(confidence)
 272:                 .source(SentimentProviderName.normalize(source))
 273:                 .summary(summary)
 274:                 .observedAt(Instant.now())
 275:                 .build());
 276:     }
 277: 
 278:     private String normalizeSymbol(String symbol) {
 279:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) throw new IllegalArgumentException("symbol is required");
 280:         return symbol.trim().toUpperCase();
 281:     }
 282: 
 283:     private void validateRange(BigDecimal value, String field) {
 284:         if (value == null || value.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(-1)) < 0 || value.compareTo(BigDecimal.ONE) > 0) {
 285:             throw new IllegalArgumentException(field + " must be between -1 and 1");
 286:         }
 287:     }
 288: 
 289:     private void validateConfidence(BigDecimal value) {
 290:         if (value == null || value.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) < 0 || value.compareTo(BigDecimal.ONE) > 0) {
 291:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("confidence must be between 0 and 1");
 292:         }
 293:     }
 294: 
 295:     private String abbreviate(String value, int limit) {
 296:         if (value == null) return null;
 297:         return value.length() <= limit ? value : value.substring(0, limit - 3) + "...";
 298:     }
 299: 
 300:     private record ProviderAggregate(
 301:             BigDecimal score,
 302:             BigDecimal confidence,
 303:             int sampleCount,
 304:             Instant latestObservedAt
 305:     ) {}
 306: 
 307:     private record Aggregation(
 308:             BigDecimal score,
 309:             int sampleCount,
 310:             List<ProviderSentiment> providers
 311:     ) {}
 312: }

### src/main/java/com/crypto/service/OrderBookLiquidityService.java
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   4: import java.math.MathContext;
   5: import java.math.RoundingMode;
   6: import java.time.Duration;
   7: import java.time.Instant;
   8: import java.util.ArrayDeque;
   9: import java.util.ArrayList;
  10: import java.util.Comparator;
  11: import java.util.Deque;
  12: import java.util.List;
  13: import java.util.Map;
  14: import java.util.Set;
  15: import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
  16: import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
  17: import java.util.concurrent.RejectedExecutionException;
  18: 
  19: import org.slf4j.Logger;
  20: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
  21: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
  22: import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
  23: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
  24: 
  25: import com.crypto.administration.service.CoinConfigurationService;
  26: import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
  27: import com.crypto.client.binance.dto.BinanceOrderBook;
  28: import com.crypto.client.config.binance.BinanceMarketDataProperties;
  29: import com.crypto.config.OrderBookProperties;
  30: import com.crypto.config.OrderBookProperties.IntervalPolicy;
  31: import com.crypto.domain.LiquidityContextStatus;
  32: import com.crypto.domain.SignalDecision;
  33: import com.crypto.dto.OrderBookLevel;
  34: import com.crypto.dto.OrderBookLiquidityResult;
  35: import com.crypto.dto.OrderBookSnapshot;
  36: import com.crypto.market.service.OrderBookSnapshotService;
  37: 
  38: @Service
  39: public class OrderBookLiquidityService {
  40: 
  41:     private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(OrderBookLiquidityService.class);
  42:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  43:     private static final BigDecimal ONE_HUNDRED = BigDecimal.valueOf(100);
  44: 
  45:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient marketDataClient;
  46:     private final BinanceMarketDataProperties marketDataProperties;
  47:     private final OrderBookProperties properties;
  48:     private final CoinConfigurationService coinConfigurationService;
  49:     private final OrderBookSnapshotService orderBookSnapshotService;
  50:     private final Executor collectionExecutor;
  51:     private final Map<String, Deque<OrderBookSnapshot>> historyBySymbol = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
  52: 
  53:     // FIX-11E: shared single-flight guard protects scheduled and live fallback collection.
  54:     // Golden rule: Replay = Production. Acquisition concurrency changes here; the shared
  55:     // normalized Order Book evaluation consumed by Production and Replay does not.
  56:     private final Set<String> inFlight = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  57: 
  58:     public OrderBookLiquidityService(
  59:             BinanceMarketDataClient marketDataClient,
  60:             BinanceMarketDataProperties marketDataProperties,
  61:             OrderBookProperties properties,
  62:             CoinConfigurationService coinConfigurationService,
  63:             OrderBookSnapshotService orderBookSnapshotService,
  64:             @Qualifier("orderBookCollectionExecutor") Executor collectionExecutor
  65:     ) {
  66:         this.marketDataClient = marketDataClient;
  67:         this.marketDataProperties = marketDataProperties;
  68:         this.properties = properties;
  69:         this.coinConfigurationService = coinConfigurationService;
  70:         this.orderBookSnapshotService = orderBookSnapshotService;
  71:         this.collectionExecutor = collectionExecutor;
  72:     }
  73: 
  74:     @Scheduled(fixedDelayString = "${analysis.order-book.snapshot-interval-ms:5000}")
  75:     public void collectConfiguredOrderBooks() {
  76:         if (!properties.enabled() || !marketDataProperties.isEnabled()) {
  77:             return;
  78:         }
  79:         coinConfigurationService.enabledSymbols().stream()
  80:                 .filter(symbol -> symbol != null && !symbol.isBlank())
  81:                 .map(String::toUpperCase)
  82:                 .distinct()
  83:                 // FIX-11E: dispatch symbols independently; one slow Binance request must not serialize the sweep.
  84:                 .forEach(symbol -> {
  85:                     try {
  86:                         collectionExecutor.execute(() -> collectSafely(symbol));
  87:                     } catch (RejectedExecutionException exception) {
  88:                         // Do not run Binance network work on the scheduler thread under saturation.
  89:                         log.warn("FIX-11E Order Book collection rejected because executor is saturated: symbol={}", symbol);
  90:                     }
  91:                 });
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     /** Backward-compatible entry point used while building pre-strategy market context. */
  95:     public OrderBookLiquidityResult evaluate(
  96:             String symbol,
  97:             SignalDecision currentDecision,
  98:             boolean entryAllowed,
  99:             BigDecimal currentPrice,
 100:             BigDecimal stopLoss,
 101:             BigDecimal takeProfit,
 102:             Instant evaluatedAt
 103:     ) {
 104:         return evaluate(symbol, null, currentDecision, entryAllowed,
 105:                 currentPrice, stopLoss, takeProfit, evaluatedAt);
 106:     }
 107: 
 108:     /**
 109:      * FIX-112C historical replay path. Replay reads persisted Production Order Book
 110:      * observations strictly as-of the historical evaluation timestamp and routes them
 111:      * through the same normalized evaluator used by live Production. Pre-V74 windows
 112:      * with no persisted evidence remain explicitly UNAVAILABLE.
 113:      */
 114:     public OrderBookLiquidityResult evaluateHistorical(
 115:             String symbol,
 116:             String interval,
 117:             SignalDecision currentDecision,
 118:             boolean entryAllowed,
 119:             BigDecimal currentPrice,
 120:             BigDecimal stopLoss,
 121:             BigDecimal takeProfit,
 122:             Instant evaluatedAt
 123:     ) {
 124:         IntervalPolicy policy = properties.policyFor(interval == null ? "5m" : interval);
 125:         Instant snapshotTime = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
 126:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
 127:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 128:                     entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 129:                     false, false, 0, "Order-book liquidity analysis is disabled.", snapshotTime,
 130:                     policy, 0L);
 131:         }
 132: 
 133:         // FIX-112C: historical Replay reads only persisted Production observations
 134:         // whose observed_at is inside the exact as-of window. No live Binance book
 135:         // and no future observation can enter a historical decision.
 136:         Instant windowStart = snapshotTime.minusSeconds(policy.windowSeconds());
 137:         List<NormalizedObservation> observations = orderBookSnapshotService
 138:                 .find(symbol, windowStart, snapshotTime).stream()
 139:                 .map(row -> new NormalizedObservation(row.observedAt(),
 140:                         // FIX-112C parity preservation: an invalid persisted snapshot must remain
 141:                         // in the observation sequence. Pre-FIX-112 Production counted every snapshot
 142:                         // in the active window and, critically, returned UNAVAILABLE when the latest
 143:                         // snapshot itself had no valid midpoint. Dropping invalid rows here would
 144:                         // silently change that established Production behavior during Replay.
 145:                         row.bestBid() == null || row.bestAsk() == null
 146:                                 || row.bestBid().add(row.bestAsk()).signum() <= 0
 147:                                 ? null
 148:                                 : new SnapshotMetrics(
 149:                                         row.bidDepth(), row.askDepth(), row.depthImbalance(),
 150:                                         row.spreadBps() == null ? null : row.spreadBps().divide(ONE_HUNDRED, MC),
 151:                                         row.bidWallPrice(), row.bidWallQuantity(),
 152:                                         row.askWallPrice(), row.askWallQuantity())))
 153:                 .toList();
 154:         if (observations.isEmpty()) {
 155:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.UNAVAILABLE, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 156:                     entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 157:                     false, false, 0,
 158:                     "No persisted Production order-book observation exists for this historical replay window; live data was not substituted.",
 159:                     snapshotTime, policy, 0L);
 160:         }
 161:         return evaluateNormalized(symbol, interval, currentDecision, entryAllowed,
 162:                 currentPrice, stopLoss, takeProfit, snapshotTime, policy, observations);
 163:     }
 164: 
 165:     public OrderBookLiquidityResult evaluate(
 166:             String symbol,
 167:             String interval,
 168:             SignalDecision currentDecision,
 169:             boolean entryAllowed,
 170:             BigDecimal currentPrice,
 171:             BigDecimal stopLoss,
 172:             BigDecimal takeProfit,
 173:             Instant evaluatedAt
 174:     ) {
 175:         IntervalPolicy policy = properties.policyFor(interval == null ? "5m" : interval);
 176:         Instant snapshotTime = evaluatedAt == null ? Instant.now() : evaluatedAt;
 177:         if (!properties.enabled()) {
 178:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.DISABLED, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 179:                     entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 180:                     false, false, 0, "Order-book liquidity analysis is disabled.", snapshotTime,
 181:                     policy, 0L);
 182:         }
 183: 
 184:         String normalizedSymbol = normalize(symbol);
 185:         Deque<OrderBookSnapshot> deque = historyBySymbol.get(normalizedSymbol);
 186:         if (deque == null || deque.isEmpty()) {
 187:             collectSafely(normalizedSymbol);
 188:             deque = historyBySymbol.get(normalizedSymbol);
 189:         }
 190:         if (deque == null || deque.isEmpty()) {
 191:             // FIX-091 / Fix 2: in LIVE analysis, zero observations are not proof that liquidity is safe.
 192:             // Hold a fresh entry until the configured sample minimum is reached. Historical replay uses
 193:             // evaluateHistorical(...) and deliberately keeps its separate UNAVAILABLE limitation.
 194:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 195:                     false, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 196:                     false, false, 0, "Live order-book sampling is not ready (0/" + policy.minimumObservations()
 197:                             + "). Fresh entry is held until enough observations are collected.", snapshotTime,
 198:                     policy, 0L);
 199:         }
 200: 
 201:         List<OrderBookSnapshot> snapshots;
 202:         synchronized (deque) {
 203:             Instant windowStart = snapshotTime.minusSeconds(policy.windowSeconds());
 204:             snapshots = deque.stream()
 205:                     .filter(snapshot -> !snapshot.capturedAt().isAfter(snapshotTime))
 206:                     .filter(snapshot -> !snapshot.capturedAt().isBefore(windowStart))
 207:                     .toList();
 208:         }
 209:         if (snapshots.isEmpty()) {
 210:             // FIX-091 / Fix 2: an enabled LIVE order-book service with no observation in the active
 211:             // window is an insufficient-sampling condition, not a neutral PASS.
 212:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 213:                     false, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 214:                     false, false, 0,
 215:                     "No live order-book observation exists inside the " + policy.windowSeconds()
 216:                             + " second window for interval " + interval + "; fresh entry is held until "
 217:                             + policy.minimumObservations() + " observations are available.",
 218:                     snapshotTime, policy, 0L);
 219:         }
 220: 
 221:         // FIX-112C parity preservation: keep every live snapshot in sequence and let the
 222:         // shared evaluator enforce the exact pre-refactor latest-snapshot validity gate.
 223:         // Do not filter individual invalid snapshots here; doing so changed live Production
 224:         // semantics by allowing an older valid snapshot to replace an invalid latest reading.
 225:         List<NormalizedObservation> observations = snapshots.stream()
 226:                 .map(snapshot -> {
 227:                     BigDecimal mid = midpoint(snapshot);
 228:                     return new NormalizedObservation(snapshot.capturedAt(),
 229:                             mid == null || mid.signum() <= 0 ? null : metrics(snapshot, mid));
 230:                 })
 231:                 .toList();
 232:         return evaluateNormalized(symbol, interval, currentDecision, entryAllowed,
 233:                 currentPrice, stopLoss, takeProfit, snapshotTime, policy, observations);
 234:     }
 235: 
 236:     /**
 237:      * FIX-112C shared decision path. Production normalizes live snapshots into this
 238:      * representation; Replay loads the same normalized observations from MySQL.
 239:      * All status/veto/wall-persistence logic below is therefore identical.
 240:      */
 241:     private OrderBookLiquidityResult evaluateNormalized(
 242:             String symbol, String interval, SignalDecision currentDecision, boolean entryAllowed,
 243:             BigDecimal currentPrice, BigDecimal stopLoss, BigDecimal takeProfit,
 244:             Instant snapshotTime, IntervalPolicy policy,
 245:             List<NormalizedObservation> observations) {
 246:         int observationCount = observations.size();
 247:         long observedSeconds = observedDurationSecondsNormalized(observations);
 248: 
 249:         // FIX-112C PRODUCTION-BEHAVIOR PRESERVATION:
 250:         // Before the shared-evaluator refactor, live Production inspected ONLY the latest
 251:         // snapshot's midpoint first. If that latest reading was invalid, the entire Order Book
 252:         // evaluation returned UNAVAILABLE even when older snapshots in the window were valid.
 253:         // Keep that exact gate explicitly in the shared path so Replay conforms to Production;
 254:         // tolerating/filtering an invalid latest snapshot is a separate trading-behavior change
 255:         // and must never ride along inside this parity/infrastructure fix.
 256:         SnapshotMetrics latestMetrics = observations.get(observationCount - 1).metrics();
 257:         if (latestMetrics == null) {
 258:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.UNAVAILABLE, currentDecision, currentDecision,
 259:                     entryAllowed, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
 260:                     false, false, observationCount, "Order-book best bid/ask is unavailable.",
 261:                     snapshotTime, policy, observedSeconds);
 262:         }
 263: 
 264:         if (observationCount < policy.minimumObservations()) {
 265:             // FIX-112C exact parity: once historical observations exist, partial sampling
 266:             // must reproduce Production's INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD rather than becoming a
 267:             // Replay-only UNAVAILABLE pass. Only a completely absent historical window is
 268:             // reported UNAVAILABLE by evaluateHistorical().
 269:             return result(LiquidityContextStatus.INSUFFICIENT_DATA_HOLD,
 270:                     currentDecision, currentDecision, false,
 271:                     latestMetrics.imbalance(), latestMetrics.bidDepth(), latestMetrics.askDepth(),
 272:                     latestMetrics.spreadPercent(), latestMetrics.bidWallPrice(), latestMetrics.bidWallSize(),
 273:                     latestMetrics.askWallPrice(), latestMetrics.askWallSize(), false, false,
 274:                     observationCount,
 275:                     "Collecting " + interval + " liquidity observations (" + observationCount + "/"
 276:                             + policy.minimumObservations() + ") inside a " + policy.windowSeconds()
 277:                             + " second window. Fresh entry is held until the minimum sample is ready.",
 278:                     snapshotTime, policy, observedSeconds);
 279:         }
 280: 
 281:         PersistentWall bidWall = persistentWallNormalized(observations, true, policy);
 282:         PersistentWall askWall = persistentWallNormalized(observations, false, policy);
 283:         boolean targetBlocked = isBuy(currentDecision)
 284:                 && currentPrice != null && takeProfit != null && askWall != null
 285:                 && askWall.price().compareTo(currentPrice) > 0
 286:                 && askWall.price().compareTo(takeProfit) <= 0;
 287:         boolean stopExposed = isBuy(currentDecision)
 288:                 && stopLoss != null && (bidWall == null || bidWall.price().compareTo(stopLoss) < 0);
 289: 
 290:         WallLifecycle askWallLifecycle = wallLifecycle(askWall, currentPrice, latestMetrics.imbalance(), policy);
 291:         LiquidityContextStatus status = classify(latestMetrics.imbalance(), targetBlocked, stopExposed, askWallLifecycle);
 292:         boolean finalEntryAllowed = entryAllowed;
 293:         SignalDecision finalDecision = currentDecision;
 294:         String explanation = explanation(status, latestMetrics, bidWall, askWall,
 295:                 targetBlocked, stopExposed, interval, policy, observedSeconds, askWallLifecycle);
 296: 
 297:         boolean strongBearishImbalance = latestMetrics.imbalance() != null
 298:                 && latestMetrics.imbalance().compareTo(properties.strongImbalance().negate()) <= 0;
 299:         boolean ultraCloseWallBeingConsumed = targetBlocked
 300:                 && askWallLifecycle != null
 301:                 && askWallLifecycle.distancePercent().compareTo(new BigDecimal("0.10")) <= 0
 302:                 && askWallLifecycle.sizeChangePercent().compareTo(new BigDecimal("-10.0")) <= 0;
 303:         boolean hardTargetBlock = targetBlocked
 304:                 && askWallLifecycle != null
 305:                 && askWallLifecycle.strengthScore() >= 70
 306:                 && askWallLifecycle.trend() != WallTrend.WEAKENING
 307:                 && !ultraCloseWallBeingConsumed;
 308:         boolean strongConflict = hardTargetBlock || strongBearishImbalance;
 309:         if (isBuy(currentDecision)
 310:                 && properties.vetoStrongConflict()
 311:                 && policy.allowVeto()
 312:                 && policy.influence().compareTo(new BigDecimal("0.50")) >= 0
 313:                 && strongConflict) {
 314:             finalEntryAllowed = false;
 315:             finalDecision = SignalDecision.WATCH;
 316:             explanation += " The " + interval
 317:                     + " policy permits a hard veto because liquidity pressure is strong enough to matter now; the long entry was downgraded to WATCH.";
 318:         } else if (targetBlocked && !hardTargetBlock) {
 319:             if (ultraCloseWallBeingConsumed) {
 320:                 explanation += " The target-side wall is ultra-close but is already shrinking materially, so it is treated as potentially consumable breakout liquidity rather than an automatic hard veto. It remains negative execution evidence and fresh confirmation is still required.";
 321:             } else {
 322:                 explanation += " The wall is relevant to the target but is not strong/stable enough for a hard veto. It remains execution evidence and may reduce confidence while fresh signals continue to be evaluated.";
 323:             }
 324:         } else if (strongConflict && !policy.allowVeto()) {
 325:             explanation += " This interval is informational only for liquidity; no entry veto was applied.";
 326:         }
 327: 
 328:         return result(status, currentDecision, finalDecision, finalEntryAllowed,
 329:                 latestMetrics.imbalance(), latestMetrics.bidDepth(), latestMetrics.askDepth(),
 330:                 latestMetrics.spreadPercent(), bidWall == null ? null : bidWall.price(),
 331:                 bidWall == null ? null : bidWall.size(), askWall == null ? null : askWall.price(),
 332:                 askWall == null ? null : askWall.size(), targetBlocked, stopExposed,
 333:                 observationCount, explanation, snapshotTime, policy,
 334:                 Math.max(bidWall == null ? 0L : bidWall.persistenceSeconds(),
 335:                         askWall == null ? 0L : askWall.persistenceSeconds()));
 336:     }
 337: 
 338:     private PersistentWall persistentWallNormalized(List<NormalizedObservation> observations, boolean bid, IntervalPolicy policy) {
 339:         List<WallObservation> candidates = observations.stream()
 340:                 .map(observation -> {
 341:                     SnapshotMetrics metrics = observation.metrics();
 342:                     // Pre-FIX-112 persistentWall(...) skipped an individual older snapshot when
 343:                     // its midpoint was invalid. Preserve that behavior while the latest-snapshot
 344:                     // validity gate above remains authoritative for the overall evaluation.
 345:                     if (metrics == null) return null;
 346:                     BigDecimal price = bid ? metrics.bidWallPrice() : metrics.askWallPrice();
 347:                     BigDecimal size = bid ? metrics.bidWallSize() : metrics.askWallSize();
 348:                     return price == null ? null : new WallObservation(observation.capturedAt(), price, size);
 349:                 })
 350:                 .filter(java.util.Objects::nonNull)
 351:                 .toList();
 352:         if (candidates.size() < policy.minimumObservations()) return null;
 353:         WallObservation latest = candidates.get(candidates.size() - 1);
 354:         List<WallObservation> matching = candidates.stream()
 355:                 .filter(observation -> percentDistance(observation.price(), latest.price())
 356:                         .compareTo(properties.wallPriceTolerancePercent()) <= 0)
 357:                 .toList();
 358:         if (matching.size() < policy.minimumObservations()) return null;
 359:         long persistenceSeconds = Duration.between(matching.get(0).capturedAt(), matching.get(matching.size() - 1).capturedAt()).toSeconds();
 360:         if (persistenceSeconds < policy.minimumWallPersistenceSeconds()) return null;
 361:         BigDecimal avgSize = matching.stream().map(WallObservation::size).reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add)
 362:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(matching.size()), MC);
 363:         BigDecimal firstSize = matching.get(0).size();
 364:         BigDecimal latestSize = matching.get(matching.size() - 1).size();
 365:         BigDecimal sizeChangePercent = firstSize == null || firstSize.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
 366:                 : latestSize.subtract(firstSize).divide(firstSize, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(ONE_HUNDRED);
 367:         return new PersistentWall(latest.price(), avgSize, latestSize, firstSize, sizeChangePercent,
 368:                 matching.size(), persistenceSeconds);
 369:     }
 370: 
 371:     private long observedDurationSecondsNormalized(List<NormalizedObservation> observations) {
 372:         if (observations == null || observations.size() < 2) return 0L;
 373:         return Math.max(0L, Duration.between(observations.get(0).capturedAt(),
 374:                 observations.get(observations.size() - 1).capturedAt()).toSeconds());
 375:     }
 376: 
 377:     private void collectSafely(String symbol) {
 378:         // FIX-11E: guard the actual collection boundary because evaluate() also calls this method directly.
 379:         if (!inFlight.add(symbol)) {
 380:             log.debug("FIX-11E Order Book collection already in-flight: symbol={}", symbol);
 381:             return;
 382:         }
 383:         long collectionStartedNanos = System.nanoTime();
 384:         try {
 385:             BinanceOrderBook raw = marketDataClient.getOrderBook(symbol, properties.depthLimit());
 386:             OrderBookSnapshot snapshot = new OrderBookSnapshot(
 387:                     symbol,
 388:                     Instant.now(),
 389:                     raw.lastUpdateId(),
 390:                     mapLevels(raw.bids()),
 391:                     mapLevels(raw.asks())
 392:             );
 393:             Deque<OrderBookSnapshot> deque = historyBySymbol.computeIfAbsent(symbol, key -> new ArrayDeque<>());
 394:             synchronized (deque) {
 395:                 deque.addLast(snapshot);
 396:                 Instant oldestAllowed = snapshot.capturedAt()
 397:                         .minusSeconds(properties.maximumWindowSeconds() + 60L);
 398:                 while (!deque.isEmpty() && (deque.peekFirst().capturedAt().isBefore(oldestAllowed)
 399:                         || deque.size() > properties.historySize())) {
 400:                     deque.removeFirst();
 401:                 }
 402:             }
 403: 
 404:             // FIX-112C: persist EVERY collected snapshot, including a snapshot whose latest
 405:             // bid/ask is invalid. That invalid observation is trading-relevant evidence because
 406:             // established Production behavior returns UNAVAILABLE when the latest midpoint cannot
 407:             // be formed. Replay cannot reproduce that behavior if invalid snapshots disappear
 408:             // during persistence.
 409:             BigDecimal mid = midpoint(snapshot);
 410:             SnapshotMetrics m = mid == null || mid.signum() <= 0 ? null : metrics(snapshot, mid);
 411:             int latencyMs = (int) Math.min(Integer.MAX_VALUE,
 412:                     java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit.NANOSECONDS.toMillis(System.nanoTime() - collectionStartedNanos));
 413:             orderBookSnapshotService.recordAsync(symbol, snapshot.capturedAt(),
 414:                     snapshot.bids().isEmpty() ? null : snapshot.bids().get(0).price(),
 415:                     snapshot.asks().isEmpty() ? null : snapshot.asks().get(0).price(),
 416:                     m == null ? null : m.spreadPercent(),
 417:                     m == null ? null : m.bidDepth(),
 418:                     m == null ? null : m.askDepth(),
 419:                     m == null ? null : m.imbalance(),
 420:                     m == null ? null : m.bidWallPrice(),
 421:                     m == null ? null : m.bidWallSize(),
 422:                     m == null ? null : m.askWallPrice(),
 423:                     m == null ? null : m.askWallSize(), latencyMs);
 424:         } catch (Exception exception) {
 425:             log.warn("Unable to collect order book for {}: {}", symbol, exception.getMessage());
 426:         } finally {
 427:             // Always release on success/failure so a symbol cannot become permanently stuck in-flight.
 428:             inFlight.remove(symbol);
 429:         }
 430:     }
 431: 
 432:     private List<OrderBookLevel> mapLevels(List<List<String>> rows) {
 433:         if (rows == null) return List.of();
 434:         List<OrderBookLevel> levels = new ArrayList<>(rows.size());
 435:         for (List<String> row : rows) {
 436:             if (row == null || row.size() < 2) continue;
 437:             BigDecimal price = new BigDecimal(row.get(0));
 438:             BigDecimal quantity = new BigDecimal(row.get(1));
 439:             if (price.signum() > 0 && quantity.signum() > 0) {
 440:                 levels.add(new OrderBookLevel(price, quantity));
 441:             }
 442:         }
 443:         return levels;
 444:     }
 445: 
 446:     private SnapshotMetrics metrics(OrderBookSnapshot snapshot, BigDecimal mid) {
 447:         BigDecimal range = properties.rangePercent().divide(ONE_HUNDRED, MC);
 448:         BigDecimal lower = mid.multiply(BigDecimal.ONE.subtract(range), MC);
 449:         BigDecimal upper = mid.multiply(BigDecimal.ONE.add(range), MC);
 450:         List<OrderBookLevel> bids = snapshot.bids().stream()
 451:                 .filter(level -> level.price().compareTo(lower) >= 0).toList();
 452:         List<OrderBookLevel> asks = snapshot.asks().stream()
 453:                 .filter(level -> level.price().compareTo(upper) <= 0).toList();
 454:         BigDecimal bidDepth = sumNotional(bids);
 455:         BigDecimal askDepth = sumNotional(asks);
 456:         BigDecimal total = bidDepth.add(askDepth);
 457:         BigDecimal imbalance = total.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
 458:                 : bidDepth.subtract(askDepth).divide(total, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 459:         OrderBookLevel bidWall = wall(bids);
 460:         OrderBookLevel askWall = wall(asks);
 461:         return new SnapshotMetrics(bidDepth, askDepth, imbalance, spreadPercent(snapshot),
 462:                 bidWall == null ? null : bidWall.price(), bidWall == null ? null : bidWall.notional(),
 463:                 askWall == null ? null : askWall.price(), askWall == null ? null : askWall.notional());
 464:     }
 465: 
 466:     private PersistentWall persistentWall(
 467:             List<OrderBookSnapshot> snapshots,
 468:             boolean bid,
 469:             IntervalPolicy policy
 470:     ) {
 471:         List<WallObservation> candidates = snapshots.stream()
 472:                 .map(snapshot -> {
 473:                     BigDecimal mid = midpoint(snapshot);
 474:                     if (mid == null) return null;
 475:                     SnapshotMetrics metrics = metrics(snapshot, mid);
 476:                     BigDecimal price = bid ? metrics.bidWallPrice() : metrics.askWallPrice();
 477:                     BigDecimal size = bid ? metrics.bidWallSize() : metrics.askWallSize();
 478:                     return price == null ? null : new WallObservation(snapshot.capturedAt(), price, size);
 479:                 })
 480:                 .filter(observation -> observation != null)
 481:                 .toList();
 482:         if (candidates.size() < policy.minimumObservations()) return null;
 483: 
 484:         WallObservation latest = candidates.get(candidates.size() - 1);
 485:         List<WallObservation> matching = candidates.stream()
 486:                 .filter(observation -> percentDistance(observation.price(), latest.price())
 487:                         .compareTo(properties.wallPriceTolerancePercent()) <= 0)
 488:                 .toList();
 489:         if (matching.size() < policy.minimumObservations()) return null;
 490:         long persistenceSeconds = Duration.between(
 491:                 matching.get(0).capturedAt(), matching.get(matching.size() - 1).capturedAt()).toSeconds();
 492:         if (persistenceSeconds < policy.minimumWallPersistenceSeconds()) return null;
 493:         BigDecimal avgSize = matching.stream().map(WallObservation::size)
 494:                 .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add)
 495:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(matching.size()), MC);
 496:         BigDecimal firstSize = matching.get(0).size();
 497:         BigDecimal latestSize = matching.get(matching.size() - 1).size();
 498:         BigDecimal sizeChangePercent = firstSize == null || firstSize.signum() == 0
 499:                 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
 500:                 : latestSize.subtract(firstSize)
 501:                         .divide(firstSize, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 502:                         .multiply(ONE_HUNDRED);
 503:         return new PersistentWall(latest.price(), avgSize, latestSize, firstSize, sizeChangePercent,
 504:                 matching.size(), persistenceSeconds);
 505:     }
 506: 
 507: 
 508:     private WallLifecycle wallLifecycle(
 509:             PersistentWall wall,
 510:             BigDecimal currentPrice,
 511:             BigDecimal imbalance,
 512:             IntervalPolicy policy
 513:     ) {
 514:         if (wall == null || currentPrice == null || currentPrice.signum() <= 0) return null;
 515: 
 516:         BigDecimal change = wall.sizeChangePercent() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : wall.sizeChangePercent();
 517:         WallTrend trend = change.compareTo(new BigDecimal("-20")) <= 0
 518:                 ? WallTrend.WEAKENING
 519:                 : change.compareTo(new BigDecimal("20")) >= 0 ? WallTrend.GROWING : WallTrend.STABLE;
 520: 
 521:         BigDecimal distance = percentDistance(wall.price(), currentPrice);
 522:         int persistenceScore;
 523:         long minimumPersistence = Math.max(1L, policy.minimumWallPersistenceSeconds());
 524:         double persistenceRatio = Math.min(2.0, wall.persistenceSeconds() / (double) minimumPersistence);
 525:         persistenceScore = (int) Math.round(35.0 * (persistenceRatio / 2.0));
 526: 
 527:         int trendScore = switch (trend) {
 528:             case GROWING -> 30;
 529:             case STABLE -> 20;
 530:             case WEAKENING -> 5;
 531:         };
 532: 
 533:         int proximityScore = distance.compareTo(new BigDecimal("0.10")) <= 0 ? 25
 534:                 : distance.compareTo(new BigDecimal("0.25")) <= 0 ? 20
 535:                 : distance.compareTo(new BigDecimal("0.50")) <= 0 ? 10 : 5;
 536:         int imbalanceScore = imbalance != null && imbalance.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) < 0 ? 10 : 0;
 537:         int strength = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, persistenceScore + trendScore + proximityScore + imbalanceScore));
 538: 
 539:         return new WallLifecycle(trend, strength, change, distance);
 540:     }
 541: 
 542:     private OrderBookLevel wall(List<OrderBookLevel> levels) {
 543:         if (levels.isEmpty()) return null;
 544:         BigDecimal average = sumNotional(levels).divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(levels.size()), MC);
 545:         BigDecimal threshold = average.multiply(properties.wallSizeMultiplier(), MC);
 546:         return levels.stream().filter(level -> level.notional().compareTo(threshold) >= 0)
 547:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(OrderBookLevel::notional)).orElse(null);
 548:     }
 549: 
 550:     private LiquidityContextStatus classify(
 551:             BigDecimal imbalance,
 552:             boolean targetBlocked,
 553:             boolean stopExposed,
 554:             WallLifecycle askWallLifecycle
 555:     ) {
 556:         if (targetBlocked) {
 557:             if (askWallLifecycle != null && askWallLifecycle.trend() == WallTrend.WEAKENING) {
 558:                 return LiquidityContextStatus.WALL_WEAKENING;
 559:             }
 560:             return LiquidityContextStatus.TARGET_BLOCKED;
 561:         }
 562:         if (imbalance.compareTo(properties.strongImbalance().negate()) <= 0) {
 563:             return LiquidityContextStatus.BEARISH_PRESSURE;
 564:         }
 565:         if (imbalance.compareTo(properties.moderateImbalance()) >= 0) {
 566:             return LiquidityContextStatus.BULLISH_SUPPORT;
 567:         }
 568:         if (stopExposed) return LiquidityContextStatus.STOP_EXPOSED;
 569:         return LiquidityContextStatus.BALANCED;
 570:     }
 571: 
 572:     private String explanation(
 573:             LiquidityContextStatus status,
 574:             SnapshotMetrics metrics,
 575:             PersistentWall bidWall,
 576:             PersistentWall askWall,
 577:             boolean targetBlocked,
 578:             boolean stopExposed,
 579:             String interval,
 580:             IntervalPolicy policy,
 581:             long observedSeconds,
 582:             WallLifecycle askWallLifecycle
 583:     ) {
 584:         StringBuilder text = new StringBuilder("Order-book status ").append(status)
 585:                 .append(" for ").append(interval).append(" using a ")
 586:                 .append(policy.windowSeconds()).append(" second window, influence ")
 587:                 .append(policy.influence()).append(", veto ")
 588:                 .append(policy.allowVeto() ? "enabled" : "disabled")
 589:                 .append(". Observed ").append(observedSeconds).append(" seconds. Imbalance ")
 590:                 .append(metrics.imbalance().setScale(3, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)).append(".");
 591:         if (askWall != null) {
 592:             text.append(" Persistent ask wall at ").append(askWall.price())
 593:                     .append(" persisted ").append(askWall.persistenceSeconds()).append(" seconds.");
 594:             if (askWallLifecycle != null) {
 595:                 text.append(" Wall lifecycle=").append(askWallLifecycle.trend())
 596:                         .append(", strength=").append(askWallLifecycle.strengthScore()).append("/100")
 597:                         .append(", size change=").append(askWallLifecycle.sizeChangePercent().setScale(1, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)).append("%")
 598:                         .append(", distance=").append(askWallLifecycle.distancePercent().setScale(3, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)).append("%.");
 599:             }
 600:         }
 601:         if (bidWall != null) {
 602:             text.append(" Persistent bid wall at ").append(bidWall.price())
 603:                     .append(" persisted ").append(bidWall.persistenceSeconds()).append(" seconds.");
 604:         }
 605:         if (targetBlocked) text.append(" The ask wall is positioned before the proposed take-profit.");
 606:         if (stopExposed) text.append(" No persistent bid support was detected above the stop-loss.");
 607:         return text.toString();
 608:     }
 609: 
 610:     private BigDecimal midpoint(OrderBookSnapshot snapshot) {
 611:         if (snapshot.bids().isEmpty() || snapshot.asks().isEmpty()) return null;
 612:         return snapshot.bids().get(0).price().add(snapshot.asks().get(0).price())
 613:                 .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(2), MC);
 614:     }
 615: 
 616:     private BigDecimal spreadPercent(OrderBookSnapshot snapshot) {
 617:         if (snapshot.bids().isEmpty() || snapshot.asks().isEmpty()) return null;
 618:         BigDecimal bid = snapshot.bids().get(0).price();
 619:         BigDecimal ask = snapshot.asks().get(0).price();
 620:         BigDecimal mid = bid.add(ask).divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(2), MC);
 621:         return ask.subtract(bid).divide(mid, 10, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(ONE_HUNDRED);
 622:     }
 623: 
 624:     private BigDecimal sumNotional(List<OrderBookLevel> levels) {
 625:         return levels.stream().map(OrderBookLevel::notional).reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);
 626:     }
 627: 
 628:     private BigDecimal percentDistance(BigDecimal left, BigDecimal right) {
 629:         if (right == null || right.signum() == 0) return ONE_HUNDRED;
 630:         return left.subtract(right).abs().divide(right, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(ONE_HUNDRED);
 631:     }
 632: 
 633:     private boolean isBuy(SignalDecision decision) {
 634:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 635:     }
 636: 
 637:     private String normalize(String symbol) {
 638:         return symbol == null ? "" : symbol.trim().toUpperCase();
 639:     }
 640: 
 641:     private long observedDurationSeconds(List<OrderBookSnapshot> snapshots) {
 642:         if (snapshots.size() < 2) return 0L;
 643:         return Math.max(0L, Duration.between(
 644:                 snapshots.get(0).capturedAt(), snapshots.get(snapshots.size() - 1).capturedAt()).toSeconds());
 645:     }
 646: 
 647:     private OrderBookLiquidityResult result(
 648:             LiquidityContextStatus status,
 649:             SignalDecision originalDecision,
 650:             SignalDecision finalDecision,
 651:             boolean entryAllowed,
 652:             BigDecimal imbalance,
 653:             BigDecimal bidDepth,
 654:             BigDecimal askDepth,
 655:             BigDecimal spreadPercent,
 656:             BigDecimal bidWallPrice,
 657:             BigDecimal bidWallSize,
 658:             BigDecimal askWallPrice,
 659:             BigDecimal askWallSize,
 660:             boolean targetBlocked,
 661:             boolean stopExposed,
 662:             int observations,
 663:             String explanation,
 664:             Instant evaluatedAt,
 665:             IntervalPolicy policy,
 666:             long wallPersistenceSeconds
 667:     ) {
 668:         return new OrderBookLiquidityResult(status, originalDecision, finalDecision, entryAllowed,
 669:                 imbalance, bidDepth, askDepth, spreadPercent, bidWallPrice, bidWallSize,
 670:                 askWallPrice, askWallSize, targetBlocked, stopExposed, observations,
 671:                 explanation, evaluatedAt, policy.windowSeconds(), wallPersistenceSeconds,
 672:                 policy.influence(), policy.allowVeto());
 673:     }
 674: 
 675:     private record NormalizedObservation(Instant capturedAt, SnapshotMetrics metrics) {}
 676: 
 677:     private record SnapshotMetrics(
 678:             BigDecimal bidDepth,
 679:             BigDecimal askDepth,
 680:             BigDecimal imbalance,
 681:             BigDecimal spreadPercent,
 682:             BigDecimal bidWallPrice,
 683:             BigDecimal bidWallSize,
 684:             BigDecimal askWallPrice,
 685:             BigDecimal askWallSize
 686:     ) {}
 687: 
 688:     private record WallObservation(Instant capturedAt, BigDecimal price, BigDecimal size) {}
 689:     private enum WallTrend { GROWING, STABLE, WEAKENING }
 690:     private record WallLifecycle(WallTrend trend, int strengthScore, BigDecimal sizeChangePercent, BigDecimal distancePercent) {}
 691:     private record PersistentWall(
 692:             BigDecimal price,
 693:             BigDecimal size,
 694:             BigDecimal latestSize,
 695:             BigDecimal firstSize,
 696:             BigDecimal sizeChangePercent,
 697:             int observations,
 698:             long persistenceSeconds
 699:     ) {}

### src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/OrderBookSnapshotService.java
   1: package com.crypto.market.service;
   2: 
   3: import org.slf4j.Logger;
   4: import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
   5: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Qualifier;
   6: import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
   7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   8: 
   9: import java.math.BigDecimal;
  10: import java.sql.Timestamp;
  11: import java.time.Instant;
  12: import java.util.List;
  13: import java.util.Locale;
  14: import java.util.concurrent.Executor;
  15: import java.util.concurrent.RejectedExecutionException;
  16: 
  17: /**
  18:  * FIX-112C: durable normalized Order Book evidence for exact historical Replay.
  19:  * The stored imbalance is the existing signed Production formula in [-1,+1].
  20:  *
  21:  * Persistence is deliberately asynchronous and best-effort. Historical Replay parity may
  22:  * become unavailable if persistence fails, but Production sampling/decisions must remain
  23:  * unaffected by database latency, pool exhaustion, or insert errors.
  24:  */
  25: @Service
  26: public class OrderBookSnapshotService {
  27:     private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(OrderBookSnapshotService.class);
  28: 
  29:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;
  30:     private final Executor persistenceExecutor;
  31: 
  32:     public OrderBookSnapshotService(
  33:             JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate,
  34:             @Qualifier("orderBookPersistenceExecutor") Executor persistenceExecutor) {
  35:         this.jdbcTemplate = jdbcTemplate;
  36:         this.persistenceExecutor = persistenceExecutor;
  37:     }
  38: 
  39:     /**
  40:      * FIX-112C parity-safety boundary: enqueue persistence and return immediately to the
  41:      * live collector. Queue saturation is reported and the historical row is sacrificed;
  42:      * it must never execute synchronously on the Production collection thread.
  43:      */
  44:     public void recordAsync(String rawSymbol, Instant observedAt,
  45:                             BigDecimal bestBid, BigDecimal bestAsk, BigDecimal spreadPercent,
  46:                             BigDecimal bidDepth, BigDecimal askDepth, BigDecimal depthImbalance,
  47:                             BigDecimal bidWallPrice, BigDecimal bidWallQuantity,
  48:                             BigDecimal askWallPrice, BigDecimal askWallQuantity,
  49:                             int collectionLatencyMs) {
  50:         try {
  51:             persistenceExecutor.execute(() -> {
  52:                 try {
  53:                     record(rawSymbol, observedAt, bestBid, bestAsk, spreadPercent,
  54:                             bidDepth, askDepth, depthImbalance,
  55:                             bidWallPrice, bidWallQuantity, askWallPrice, askWallQuantity,
  56:                             collectionLatencyMs);
  57:                 } catch (Exception exception) {
  58:                     log.warn("Unable to persist historical order-book snapshot for {} at {}: {}. Live Production sampling is unaffected.",
  59:                             rawSymbol, observedAt, exception.getMessage());
  60:                 }
  61:             });
  62:         } catch (RejectedExecutionException exception) {
  63:             log.warn("Historical order-book persistence queue is full for {} at {}; snapshot was not persisted. Live Production sampling is unaffected.",
  64:                     rawSymbol, observedAt);
  65:         }
  66:     }
  67: 
  68:     private void record(String rawSymbol, Instant observedAt,
  69:                         BigDecimal bestBid, BigDecimal bestAsk, BigDecimal spreadPercent,
  70:                         BigDecimal bidDepth, BigDecimal askDepth, BigDecimal depthImbalance,
  71:                         BigDecimal bidWallPrice, BigDecimal bidWallQuantity,
  72:                         BigDecimal askWallPrice, BigDecimal askWallQuantity,
  73:                         int collectionLatencyMs) {
  74:         if (rawSymbol == null || rawSymbol.isBlank() || observedAt == null) return;
  75:         String symbol = rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  76:         // Existing live code expresses spread as percent. Persist bps explicitly.
  77:         BigDecimal spreadBps = spreadPercent == null ? null : spreadPercent.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
  78:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
  79:                 INSERT INTO order_book_snapshot(
  80:                     symbol, observed_at, best_bid, best_ask, spread_bps,
  81:                     bid_depth, ask_depth, depth_imbalance,
  82:                     bid_wall_price, bid_wall_quantity, ask_wall_price, ask_wall_quantity,
  83:                     collection_latency_ms, source)
  84:                 VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'BINANCE_LIVE')
  85:                 """, symbol, Timestamp.from(observedAt), bestBid, bestAsk, spreadBps,
  86:                 bidDepth, askDepth, depthImbalance,
  87:                 bidWallPrice, bidWallQuantity, askWallPrice, askWallQuantity,
  88:                 collectionLatencyMs);
  89:     }
  90: 
  91:     public List<PersistedObservation> find(String rawSymbol, Instant startInclusive, Instant endInclusive) {
  92:         String symbol = rawSymbol == null ? "" : rawSymbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  93:         return jdbcTemplate.query("""
  94:                 SELECT observed_at, best_bid, best_ask, spread_bps,
  95:                        bid_depth, ask_depth, depth_imbalance,
  96:                        bid_wall_price, bid_wall_quantity, ask_wall_price, ask_wall_quantity
  97:                 FROM order_book_snapshot
  98:                 WHERE symbol = ? AND observed_at >= ? AND observed_at <= ?
  99:                 ORDER BY observed_at ASC, id ASC
 100:                 """, (rs, rowNum) -> new PersistedObservation(
 101:                         rs.getTimestamp("observed_at").toInstant(),
 102:                         rs.getBigDecimal("best_bid"), rs.getBigDecimal("best_ask"),
 103:                         rs.getBigDecimal("spread_bps"),
 104:                         rs.getBigDecimal("bid_depth"), rs.getBigDecimal("ask_depth"),
 105:                         rs.getBigDecimal("depth_imbalance"),
 106:                         rs.getBigDecimal("bid_wall_price"), rs.getBigDecimal("bid_wall_quantity"),
 107:                         rs.getBigDecimal("ask_wall_price"), rs.getBigDecimal("ask_wall_quantity")),
 108:                 symbol, Timestamp.from(startInclusive), Timestamp.from(endInclusive));
 109:     }
 110: 
 111:     public record PersistedObservation(
 112:             Instant observedAt,
 113:             BigDecimal bestBid,
 114:             BigDecimal bestAsk,
 115:             BigDecimal spreadBps,
 116:             BigDecimal bidDepth,
 117:             BigDecimal askDepth,
 118:             BigDecimal depthImbalance,
 119:             BigDecimal bidWallPrice,
 120:             BigDecimal bidWallQuantity,
 121:             BigDecimal askWallPrice,
 122:             BigDecimal askWallQuantity) {}
