 235:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validatedSell = executionValidationService.validateSell(signal);
 236:                 if (validatedSell.allowed()) {
 237:                     ExitDecision exit = new ExitDecision(true, validatedSell.code(), validatedSell.explanation());
 238:                     BigDecimal proceeds = signal.getLatestPrice().multiply(open.quantity(), MC);
 239:                     BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(open.cost(), MC);
 240:                     BigDecimal pnlPct = percentage(open.entryPrice(), signal.getLatestPrice());
 241:                     BigDecimal totalPositionPnl = open.partialRealizedPnl().add(pnl, MC);
 242:                     BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPct = totalPositionPnlPercent(open, pnl);
 243:                     cash = cash.add(proceeds, MC);
 244:                     realized = realized.add(pnl, MC);
 245:                     trades++;
 246:                     if (totalPositionPnl.signum() > 0) wins++; else if (totalPositionPnl.signum() < 0) losses++;
 247:                     persistSell(runId, symbol, signal, open, exit, pnl, pnlPct);
 248:                     closePosition(runId, open.positionId(), signal, exit, totalPositionPnl, totalPositionPnlPct);
 249:                     replayScope.recordStop(symbol, open.positionId(), signal.getGeneratedAt(), exit.reason());
 250:                     executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(symbol, signal, exit.reason());
 251:                                         open = null;
 252:                     // Same production invocation cannot both SELL and immediately BUY again.
 253:                     continue;
 254:                 }
 255:             }
 256: 
 257:             // FIX-014 replay parity: production may use a fresh 5m BUY transition only to
 258:             // wake an already-live deferred 1m opportunity. The actual execution/risk plan
 259:             // remains the latest fresh 1m signal returned by the shared production service.
 260:             // All other non-1m signals remain context-only exactly as before.
 261:             TradeSignal executionSignal = signal;
 262:             ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision decision;
 263:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {
 264:                 int currentAllocation = open == null ? 0 : open.positionPercent();
 265:                 String currentStage = replayStage(currentAllocation);
 266:                 // FIX-026 parity: Recovery/Transition Entry is intentionally NOT reimplemented here.
 267:                 // Replay enters the exact production ExecutionIntelligenceService, which delegates to
 268:                 // RecoveryTransitionService using only candles closed as-of this historical signal.
 269:                 // This protects the ENA 12:55-13:04 KSA regression from replay-only future leakage.
 270:                 decision = executionIntelligenceService.evaluateBuy(signal, currentAllocation, currentStage);
 280:                 executionSignal = wakeup.executionSignal();
 281:                 decision = wakeup.decision();
 282:             } else {
 283:                 continue;
 284:             }
 285: 
 286:             // Warm-up evaluates the SAME production decision service so evidence/opportunity
 287:             // memory is realistic at executionStart, but it never opens or adds to a wallet
 288:             // position before the requested test window. The trigger timestamp (signal) controls
 289:             // the replay window; executionSignal owns price/risk just like production.
 290:             boolean inExecutionWindow = !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(executionStart)
 291:                     && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(executionEnd);
 292:             LocalDate signalTradeDate = signal.getGeneratedAt().atZone(ZoneOffset.UTC).toLocalDate();
 293:             if (!signalTradeDate.equals(replayTradeDate)) {
 294:                 replayTradeDate = signalTradeDate;
 295:                 replayExecutedNewPositions = 0;
 296:                 replayDailyBudget = executionSizingPolicy.initialDailyBudget(
 297:                         cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(),
 298:                         replayWalletSettings.getBaseTradeAmountUsdt(), replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions());
 299:             }
 300:             // IMPORTANT: Proven/Regression never re-implements pressure readiness, normal BUY
 301:             // routing, or FIX-014 wake-up rules. All are executed by the production
 302:             // ExecutionIntelligenceService above; only wallet/opportunity persistence is shadowed.
 303:             if (!inExecutionWindow) continue;
 304: 
 305:             if (open == null && decision.allowed()) {
 306:                 // FIX-056 Replay parity: resolve the same canonical execution-price authority
 307:                 // after the same Production decision gates, then revalidate Entry Quality at
 308:                 // that price before sizing. A replay without a fresh price event cannot claim
 309:                 // exact execution parity and therefore does not open the position here.
 310:                 ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh = executionPriceAuthorityService
 311:                         .resolve(symbol, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 312:                 if (fresh == null) continue;
 313:                 decision = executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(executionSignal, decision, fresh.price());
 314:                 if (!decision.allowed()) continue;
 315:                 int atrPercent = executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent() <= 0 ? 100 : executionSignal.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent();
 316:                 int effectivePercent = Math.max(1, Math.min(100,
 317:                         (int)Math.round(atrPercent * decision.positionPercent() / 100.0)));
 318:                 WalletExecutionSizingPolicy.Plan sizing = executionSizingPolicy.plan(
 319:                         cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(), replayDailyBudget,
 320:                         effectivePercent, 0, true, replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions(),
 321:                         replayExecutedNewPositions, fresh.price());
 322:                 BigDecimal budget = sizing.spend();
 323:                 if (sizing.allowed()) {
 324:                     BigDecimal qty = sizing.quantity();
 325:                     effectivePercent = sizing.normalizedPositionPercent();
 326:                     long positionId = openPosition(runId, symbol, executionSignal, fresh, qty, budget, effectivePercent);
 327:                     persistBuy(runId, symbol, executionSignal, fresh, qty, budget, effectivePercent, decision);
 328:                     executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(executionSignal, decision);
 329:                     cash = cash.subtract(budget, MC);
 330:                     replayExecutedNewPositions++;
 331:                     // Keep the complete immutable BUY thesis needed by production continuation.
 332:                     // Replay must not drop entry structure and silently evaluate a different exit policy.
 333:                     open = new ShadowPosition(positionId, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), fresh.price(), qty, budget,
 334:                             effectivePercent, executionSignal.getStopLoss(), executionSignal.getTakeProfit(), fresh.price(), false, null,
 335:                             ProfitLockState.INACTIVE, null,
 336:                             executionSignal.getTotalScore(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(), executionSignal.getTrendScore(), executionSignal.getTrendStructureScore(),
 337:                             executionSignal.getMomentumScore(), executionSignal.getVolumeScore(),
 338:                             NearTpState.INACTIVE, null, 0, null, false, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 339:                     // FIX-112A: only an actually opened shadow position consumes the bullish entry.
 340:                     replayScope.markEntryConsumed(executionSignal.getId());
 341:                 }
 342:             } else if (open != null && decision.allowed()) {
 343:                 // FIX-056: progressive Replay adds use the same fresh execution price and
 344:                 // execution-time Entry Quality revalidation as Production.
 345:                 ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh = executionPriceAuthorityService
 346:                         .resolve(symbol, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 347:                 if (fresh == null) continue;
 348:                 decision = executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(signal, decision, fresh.price());
 349:                 if (!decision.allowed()) continue;
 350:                 int addPercent = Math.max(1, Math.min(100 - open.positionPercent(), decision.positionPercent()));
 351:                 WalletExecutionSizingPolicy.Plan sizing = executionSizingPolicy.plan(
 352:                         cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(), replayDailyBudget,
 353:                         addPercent, open.positionPercent(), false, replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions(),
 354:                         replayExecutedNewPositions, fresh.price());
 355:                 BigDecimal budget = sizing.spend();
 356:                 if (sizing.allowed()) {
 357:                     addPercent = sizing.normalizedPositionPercent();
 358:                     BigDecimal addedQty = sizing.quantity();
 359:                     persistBuy(runId, symbol, signal, fresh, addedQty, budget, addPercent, decision);
 360:                     executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(signal, decision);
 361:                     // FIX-112A: a progressive add consumes only its own triggering signal.
 362:                     replayScope.markEntryConsumed(signal.getId());
 363:                     cash = cash.subtract(budget, MC);
 364:                     open = addToPosition(runId, open, signal, addedQty, budget, addPercent);
 365:                 }
 366:             }
 367:         }
 368:         }
 369: 
 370:         BigDecimal finalWallet = cash;
 371:         if (open != null && !timeline.isEmpty()) {
 372:             BigDecimal mark = timeline.get(timeline.size() - 1).getLatestPrice();
 373:             if (mark != null) finalWallet = finalWallet.add(mark.multiply(open.quantity(), MC), MC);
 374:         }
 375: 
 520:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation nearTp = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 521:                 nearTpState, current.entryPrice(), current.takeProfit(), price, event.observedAt(), oneMinute, five);
 522:         ShadowPosition nearTpUpdated = current.withNearTp(nearTp.state());
 523:         if (!nearTpUpdated.sameNearTp(current)) {
 524:             persistNearTpState(nearTpUpdated);
 525:         }
 526:         current = nearTpUpdated;
 527:         if (nearTp.transition()) {
 528:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay Near-TP: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, state={}, price={}, best={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, detail={}",
 529:                     runId, current.positionId(), symbol, nearTp.code(), current.nearTpState(), price,
 530:                     current.nearTpBestPrice(), nearTp.givebackPercent(), current.nearTpBearishStreak(), nearTp.explanation());
 531:         }
 532:         if (nearTp.harvestEligible()) {
 533:             BigDecimal soldQty = current.quantity().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC);
 534:             BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.Evaluation minimumCheck =
 535:                     binanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.evaluate(symbol, soldQty, price);
 536:             if (!minimumCheck.executable()) {
 537:                 // Same as Production: a below-minimum Binance order is not simulated. Reset
 538:                 // to rejection monitoring and let all existing management continue unchanged.
 539:                 current = current.withNearTp(new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 540:                         NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED, current.nearTpBestPrice(),
 541:                         0, current.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), false));
 542:                 persistNearTpState(current);
 543:                 log.info("FIX-11T Replay NEAR_TP_HARVEST_SKIPPED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, reason={}, requestedQty={}, requestedNotional={}, binanceMinimum={}; existing position management continues",
 544:                         runId, current.positionId(), symbol, minimumCheck.code(), soldQty,
 545:                         minimumCheck.requestedNotional(), minimumCheck.minimumNotional());
 546:                 return new LivePriceEvaluation(ExitDecision.hold(), current);
 547:             }
 548:             BigDecimal proceeds = soldQty.multiply(price, MC);
 549:             BigDecimal soldCost = current.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
 550:             BigDecimal partialPnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
 551:             BigDecimal partialPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : partialPnl
 552:                     .divide(soldCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 553:             current = current.withPartialHarvest(soldQty, soldCost, partialPnl);
 554:             persistNearTpPartialHarvest(runId, symbol, event, oneMinute, current, soldQty, proceeds, partialPnl, partialPct, nearTp.explanation());
 555:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, soldQty={}, remainingQty={}, price={}, partialPnl={}",
 556:                     runId, current.positionId(), symbol, soldQty, current.quantity(), price, partialPnl);
 557:             return new LivePriceEvaluation(ExitDecision.hold(), current, proceeds, partialPnl);
 558:         }
 559:         return new LivePriceEvaluation(ExitDecision.hold(), current);
 560:     }
 561: 
 562:     private ExitDecision evaluateExit(long runId, ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s, TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
 563:         BigDecimal price = s.getLatestPrice();
 564:         if (price == null) return ExitDecision.hold();
 565: 
 566:         // Replay uses the exact same temporal price-authority rule as production. A signal
 567:         // generated later may still carry a candle close from before entry; that historical
 568:         // price may update MTF context but may not retroactively hit TP/SL/profit-lock.
 569:         boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime());
 570:         if (authoritativePrice && p.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(p.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
 571:             // Exact production TP-continuation call, including immutable entry structure.
 572:             // FIX-011 regression relies on Replay and live using this same shared policy.
 573:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
 574:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
 575:                     p.entryTrend(), p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(),
 576:                     p.entryConfidence(), p.entryScore());
 577:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 578:                 BigDecimal distance = p.takeProfit().subtract(p.entryPrice());
 579:                 BigDecimal newTarget = p.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 580:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED", p.takeProfit(), newTarget, p, continuation.explanation());
 581:                 jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET take_profit_usdt=? WHERE id=?", newTarget, p.positionId());
 582:                 return new ExitDecision(false, "EXTEND_TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), newTarget);
 583:             }
 584:             oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
 585:                     runId, s.getSymbol(), p.positionId(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 586:                     p.entryTime(), p.entryPrice(), p.quantity(), p.cost(), p.stopLoss(), p.takeProfit(), p.highest(),
 587:                     p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(), p.entryScore(), p.entryConfidence(), p.entryTrend(),
 588:                     p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(), oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
 589:                     continuation.explanation());
 590:             persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), p, continuation.explanation());
 591:             return new ExitDecision(true, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation());
 592:         }
 593:         // FIX-052 fallback ordering also mirrors Production: STOP_LOSS is absolute and
 594:         // is evaluated before profit-lock. Exact V64 runs normally take this path from
 595:         // persisted live-price events rather than from signal-carried prices.
 596:         if (authoritativePrice && p.stopLoss() != null && price.compareTo(p.stopLoss()) <= 0)
 597:             return new ExitDecision(true, "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the stored stop loss.");
 598: 
 599:         ShadowPosition updated = authoritativePrice ? profitLockState(p, price) : p;
 600:         if (authoritativePrice && (updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE
 601:                 || updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 602:                 || p.profitLockActive() != updated.profitLockActive()
 603:                 || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice())
 604:                 || p.profitLockState() != updated.profitLockState())) {
 605:             BigDecimal fix118Progress = updated.takeProfit() == null || updated.takeProfit().compareTo(updated.entryPrice()) <= 0
 606:                     ? BigDecimal.ZERO
 607:                     : updated.highest().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC)
 608:                             .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 609:                             .divide(updated.takeProfit().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 610:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_PATH] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, " +
 611:                             "entry={}, target={}, highest={}, previousActive={}, active={}, previousState={}, state={}, previousLock={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, triggered={}",
 612:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 613:                     updated.entryPrice(), updated.takeProfit(), updated.highest(), p.profitLockActive(), updated.profitLockActive(),
 614:                     p.profitLockState(), updated.profitLockState(), p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice(), fix118Progress,
 615:                     updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
 616:                             && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0);
 617:         }
 618:         BigDecimal minimumProfitableExit = p.entryPrice().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005));
 619:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 620:                 && price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
 621:             String floorReason = "TP-extension rebase safety floor was breached at " + minimumProfitableExit + ". "
 622:                     + profitLockConfigText();
 623:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, floor={}",
 624:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price, minimumProfitableExit);
 625:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
 626:             return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
 627:         }
 628:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
 629:                 && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0) {
 630:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, lock={}, target={}",
 631:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 632:                     updated.profitLockPrice(), updated.takeProfit());
 633:             if (price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
 634:                 String floorReason = "Profit-lock hard floor was breached; protected profit can no longer be preserved. "
 635:                         + profitLockConfigText();
 636:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
 637:                 return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
 638:             }
 639:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(
 640:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
 641:             if (!lockDecision.exit()) {
 642:                 String holdReason = lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
 643:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, holdReason);
 644:                 return ExitDecision.hold();
 645:             }
 646:             String lockReason = "Price retraced to the protected profit level after a profitable advance. "
 647:                     + lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
 648:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, lockReason);
 649:             return new ExitDecision(true, lockDecision.code(), lockReason);
 650:         }
 651:         // Production has two SELL authorities: the live HTF PositionExitPolicy and the
 652:         // 1m signal TradeExecutionValidationService. Replay executes both shared production
 653:         // policies in the same order instead of carrying a test-only SELL rule.
 654:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(
 655:                 oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
 656:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
 657:             return new ExitDecision(true, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation());
 658:         }
 659:         if ("1m".equals(s.getInterval()) && bearish(s.getDecision())) {
 660:             TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validatedSell = executionValidationService.validateSell(s);
 661:             if (validatedSell.allowed()) {
 662:                 return new ExitDecision(true, validatedSell.code(), validatedSell.explanation());
 663:             }
 664:         }
 665:         return ExitDecision.hold();
 666:     }
 667: 
 668:     private FallbackNearTpEvaluation evaluateNearTpAtSignalFallback(long runId, ShadowPosition current,
 669:                                                                     TradeSignal signal, TradeSignal oneMinute,
 670:                                                                     TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 671:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 672:                 current.nearTpState(), current.nearTpBestPrice(), current.nearTpBearishStreak(),
 673:                 current.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), current.nearTpHarvestUsed());
 674:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 675:                 state, current.entryPrice(), current.takeProfit(), signal.getLatestPrice(), signal.getGeneratedAt(),
 676:                 oneMinute, fiveMinute);
 677:         ShadowPosition updated = current.withNearTp(evaluation.state());
 678:         if (!updated.sameNearTp(current)) persistNearTpState(updated);
 679:         if (evaluation.transition()) {
 680:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback Near-TP: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, state={}, price={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, detail={}",
 681:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), evaluation.code(), updated.nearTpState(),
 682:                     signal.getLatestPrice(), evaluation.givebackPercent(), updated.nearTpBearishStreak(), evaluation.explanation());
 683:         }
 684:         if (!evaluation.harvestEligible()) return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 685: 
 686:         BigDecimal soldQty = updated.quantity().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC);
 687:         BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.Evaluation minimumCheck =
 688:                 binanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.evaluate(signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, signal.getLatestPrice());
 689:         if (!minimumCheck.executable()) {
 690:             updated = updated.withNearTp(new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 691:                     NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED, updated.nearTpBestPrice(),
 692:                     0, updated.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), false));
 693:             persistNearTpState(updated);
 694:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_HARVEST_SKIPPED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, reason={}, requestedQty={}, requestedNotional={}, binanceMinimum={}; existing position management continues",
 695:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), minimumCheck.code(), soldQty,
 696:                     minimumCheck.requestedNotional(), minimumCheck.minimumNotional());
 697:             return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 698:         }
 699:         BigDecimal proceeds = soldQty.multiply(signal.getLatestPrice(), MC);
 700:         BigDecimal soldCost = updated.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
 701:         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
 702:         BigDecimal pnlPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : pnl.divide(soldCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 703:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 704:         updated = updated.withPartialHarvest(soldQty, soldCost, pnl);
 705:         persistNearTpPartialHarvestAtSignal(runId, signal, updated, soldQty, proceeds, pnl, pnlPct, evaluation.explanation());
 706:         log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, soldQty={}, remainingQty={}, price={}, partialPnl={}",
 707:                 runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, updated.quantity(), signal.getLatestPrice(), pnl);
 708:         return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, proceeds, pnl);
 709:     }
 710: 
 880:              neutral_count, bearish_count, evidence_score, opportunity_health, peak_score, peak_confidence, peak_decision,
 881:              peak_regime, peak_btc_status, peak_liquidity_status, peak_observed_at, recommended_position_percent,
 882:              current_final_decision, current_original_decision, five_minute_decision, one_hour_decision,
 883:              old_hard_bearish_reversal, corrected_hard_bearish_reversal, decision_code, decision_explanation)
 884:             VALUES (?, NULL, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, 0, ?, ?)
 885:             """, runId, s.getSymbol(), Timestamp.from(s.getGeneratedAt()), o.getStatus(), o.getEvidenceCount(),
 886:                 o.getBuyCount(), o.getWatchCount(), o.getNeutralCount(), o.getBearishCount(), o.getEvidenceScore(),
 887:                 o.getOpportunityHealth(), o.getPeakScore(), o.getPeakConfidence(), o.getPeakDecision(), o.getPeakRegime(),
 888:                 o.getPeakBtcStatus(), o.getPeakLiquidityStatus(), o.getPeakObservedAt() == null ? null : Timestamp.from(o.getPeakObservedAt()),
 889:                 o.getRecommendedPositionPercent(), name(s.getDecision()), name(s.getOriginalDecision()),
 890:                 o.getFiveMinuteDecision(), o.getOneHourDecision(), o.getDecisionCode(), o.getDecisionExplanation());
 891:     }

 893:     private void persistBuy(long runId,String symbol,TradeSignal s,ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh,BigDecimal qty,BigDecimal budget,int pct,ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision d){
 894:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 895:             INSERT INTO wallet_execution_test
 896:             (test_run_id,symbol,side,execution_time,execution_price,quantity,notional_usdt,position_percent,signal_interval,signal_decision,execution_source,execution_code,execution_reason)
 897:             VALUES (?,?,'BUY',?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",
 898:             runId,symbol,Timestamp.from(fresh.observedAt()),fresh.price(),qty,budget,pct,s.getInterval(),name(s.getDecision()),d.source(),d.code(),d.explanation() + " DecisionPrice=" + s.getLatestPrice() + ", executionPriceObservedAt=" + fresh.observedAt());
 899:     }
 900: 
 901:     private long openPosition(long runId,String symbol,TradeSignal s,ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh,BigDecimal qty,BigDecimal budget,int pct){
 902:         org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder kh=new org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder();
 903:         jdbcTemplate.update(c->{var ps=c.prepareStatement("""
 904:             INSERT INTO wallet_position_test
 905:             (test_run_id,symbol,status,entry_time,entry_price,quantity,total_cost_usdt,position_percent,stop_loss_usdt,take_profit_usdt,highest_price_usdt)
 906:             VALUES (?,?,'OPEN',?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",java.sql.Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS);

1018:     private BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPercent(ShadowPosition position, BigDecimal remainingLegPnl) {
1019:         BigDecimal totalPnl = position.partialRealizedPnl().add(remainingLegPnl, MC);
1020:         BigDecimal totalInvestedCost = position.cost().add(position.partialHarvestCostBasis(), MC);
1021:         return totalInvestedCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
1022:                 : totalPnl.divide(totalInvestedCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
1023:     }
1024: 
1025:     private BigDecimal percentage(BigDecimal entry,BigDecimal exit){return exit.subtract(entry).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).divide(entry,8,RoundingMode.HALF_UP);}
1026:     private boolean bullish(SignalDecision d){return d==SignalDecision.BUY||d==SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;}
1027:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d){return d==SignalDecision.SELL||d==SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;}
1028:     private String name(SignalDecision d){return d==null?null:d.name();}
1029:     private int intervalOrder(String i){return "1h".equals(i)?0:"5m".equals(i)?1:2;}
1030:     private boolean equalsNullable(BigDecimal a,BigDecimal b){return a==null?b==null:b!=null&&a.compareTo(b)==0;}

 188:             updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 74, "Running full shadow-production execution flow", null);
 189:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 190:             // FIX-052: Replay position protection consumes the same canonical live 1m
 191:             // price observations that Production consumed. Historical windows before
 192:             // V64 was deployed naturally have no events and are explicitly degraded to
 193:             // signal/candle-close protection inside ShadowProductionReplayService.

 200:                     runId, symbol, start, end, fresh.generatedSignals(), productionPriceEvents,
 201:                     () -> isStopRequested(runId));
 202:             // FIX-109: persist the parity contract with the run. A SIGNAL_PRICE_FALLBACK
 203:             // result is useful historical evidence, but must never be presented as tick-exact
 204:             // Production reproduction. Production parity is the default logic mode.
 205:             jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE analysis_test_run SET replay_price_mode=?, replay_logic_mode=? WHERE id=?",
 206:                     shadow.priceReplayMode(), shadow.logicMode(), runId);

 209: 
 210:             stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 211:             updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 82, "Comparing historical decision authority", null);
 212:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 213:             int authorityCorrections = 0;
 214:             int replaySignals = 0;
 215:             int oldHardReversals = 0;
 540:             List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles,
 541:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles,
 542:             List<Candle> oneHourCandles,
 543:             String btcSymbol,
 544:             List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles,
 545:             List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles,
 546:             List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles,
 547:             ReplayDataSource dataSource,
 548:             ReplayDataset replayDataset
 549:     ) {
 550:         List<ReplayCandle> timeline = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 551:         oneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1m", c, true)));
 552:         fiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "5m", c, true)));
 553:         oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));
 554:         btcOneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1m", c, false)));
 555:         btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
 556:         btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));
 557:         timeline.sort(java.util.Comparator
 558:                 .comparing((ReplayCandle rc) -> rc.candle().getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(rc.interval())))
 559:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> intervalOrder(rc.interval()))
 560:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> rc.primary() ? 1 : 0));
 561: 
 562:         int oneMinuteSignals = 0, fiveMinuteSignals = 0, oneHourSignals = 0;
 563:         int oneMinuteBuys = 0, fiveMinuteBuys = 0, oneHourBuys = 0;
 564:         int buys = 0, watches = 0, sells = 0, strongSells = 0, errors = 0;
 565:         java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 566: 
 567:         // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
 568:         // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
 569:         // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.
 570:         ReplayFreshSignalProfiler profiler = new ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(runId, dataSource);
 571:         // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
 572:         // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
 573:         analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 574:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
 575:         // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
 576:         // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
 577:         // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
 578:         // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
 579:         // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
 580:         for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
 581:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 582:             ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);
 583:             Candle candle = replay.candle();
 584:             Instant evaluationTime = candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(replay.interval()));
 585:             boolean inRequestedWindow = !evaluationTime.isBefore(executionStart) && !evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd);
 586:             try {
 587:                 long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 588:                 java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
 589:                 try {
 590:                     snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 591:                             ? technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 592:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), replayDataset)
 593:                             : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 594:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
 595:                 } finally {
 596:                     profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 597:                 }
 598:                 if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
 599:                     if (inRequestedWindow) {
 600:                         errors++;
 601:                         saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
 602:                                 "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
 603:                     }
 604:                     continue;
 605:                 }
 606: 
 607:                 // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
 608:                 // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
 609:                 // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.
 610:                 if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;
 611: 
 612:                 replayScope.reference(evaluationTime);
 613:                 operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 614:                 TradeSignal generated;
 615:                 try {
 616:                     generated = analysisService.analyzeForRegression(snapshot.get(), evaluationTime);
 617:                 } finally {
 618:                     profiler.recordAnalysis(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 619:                 }
 620:                 replayScope.appendSignal(generated);
 621:                 SignalDecision decision = generated.getDecision();
 622:                 if (replay.primary()) generatedSignals.add(generated);
 623: 
 624:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 625:                     if ("1m".equals(replay.interval())) { oneMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneMinuteBuys++; }
 626:                     else if ("5m".equals(replay.interval())) { fiveMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) fiveMinuteBuys++; }
 627:                     else if ("1h".equals(replay.interval())) { oneHourSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneHourBuys++; }
 628: 
 629:                     if (decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) buys++;
 630:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH) watches++;
 770:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to persist production-shaped replay TradeSignal", e);
 771:         }
 772:     }

 774:     private String snakeCase(String value) {
 775:         return value.replaceAll("([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", "$1_$2").toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
 776:     }
 777: 
 778:     private long intervalSeconds(String interval) {
 779:         return switch (interval) {
 780:             case "1m" -> 60L;
 781:             case "5m" -> 300L;
 782:             case "1h" -> 3600L;
 783:             default -> 60L;
 784:         };
 785:     }
 786: 
 787:     private int intervalOrder(String interval) {
 788:         return switch (interval) {
 789:             case "1h" -> 0;
 790:             case "5m" -> 1;
 791:             default -> 2;
 792:         };
 793:     }
 794: 
 795:     private boolean isBuy(SignalDecision decision) {
 796:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 797:     }

 799:     /**
 800:      * FIX-11L Replay-only profiler for the fresh-signal generation loop. It deliberately
 801:      * aggregates timings in memory and emits one summary log after the stage; there are no
 802:      * per-signal log writes and no Production/shared-analysis branches.
 803:      */

### src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java
 150:             // FIX-067: The wallet-managed position is the authoritative Production source for
 151:             // a dynamically extended take-profit.  paper_position historically retained the
 152:             // entry-time TP, so a fresh 1m signal could close against that stale threshold even
 153:             // after LivePositionProtectionService had persisted TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED.
 154:             // Prefer the OPEN managed TP and repair the paper snapshot when they differ. This is
 155:             // deliberately NOT a TP-policy change: it only makes both existing exit paths compare
 156:             // against the same already-approved target.
 157:             BigDecimal authoritativeTakeProfit = position.getTakeProfit();
 158:             WalletManagedPosition managedPosition = walletManagedPositionRepository
 159:                     .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
 160:                     .orElse(null);
 161:             if (managedPosition != null && managedPosition.getTakeProfitUsdt() != null) {
 162:                 authoritativeTakeProfit = managedPosition.getTakeProfitUsdt();
 163:                 if (position.getTakeProfit() == null
 164:                         || position.getTakeProfit().compareTo(authoritativeTakeProfit) != 0) {
 165:                     position.setTakeProfit(authoritativeTakeProfit);
 166:                     positionRepository.save(position);
 167:                 }
 168:             }
 169: 
 170:             if (authoritativePrice && authoritativeTakeProfit != null
 171:                     && price.compareTo(authoritativeTakeProfit) >= 0) {
 172:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 173:                         "TAKE_PROFIT", "Price reached the authoritative configured take-profit target."));
 174:             }
 175: 
 176:             if (authoritativePrice && profitLock.triggered()) {
 177:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, lock={}, progressPct={}",
 178:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, profitLock.lockPrice(), profitLock.progressPercent());
 179:                 return Optional.of(closeFromProfitLock(position, signal, profitLock));
 180:             }
 181: 
 182:             if (authoritativePrice && price.compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) <= 0) {
 183:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.STOPPED,
 184:                         "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the configured stop loss."));
 185:             }
 186: 
 187:             // FIX-118: in TP_EXTENSION_REBASE the old Profit Lock cannot execute, but the
 188:             // direct protected-profit floor still applies. Stop Loss remains absolute and
 189:             // therefore keeps priority immediately above this branch.
 190:             if (authoritativePrice && profitLock.hardProfitFloorTriggered()) {
 191:                 log.info("[FIX-118][PRODUCTION][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] signalId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, price={}, floor={}",
 192:                         signal.getId(), profitLock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, profitLock.hardProfitFloor());
 193:                 return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 194:                         "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", profitLock.explanation()));
 195:             }
 196: 
 197:             if (signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.SELL
 198:                     || signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) {
 199:                 if (profitLock.active()) {
 200:                     log.info("Normal wallet SELL suppressed because Dynamic Profit Lock is active: signalId={}, symbol={}, protectedPrice={}, currentPrice={}",
 201:                             signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), profitLock.lockPrice(), signal.getLatestPrice());
 202:                     return Optional.of(position);
 203:                 }
 204:                 TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validation =
 205:                         executionValidationService.validateSell(signal);
 206:                 if (validation.allowed()) {
 207:                     return Optional.of(closeFromSignal(position, signal, PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 208:                             signal.getDecision().name(), signal.getExplanation()));
 209:                 }
 210:                 log.info("Normal wallet SELL rejected: signalId={}, symbol={}, interval={}, reason={}, detail={}",
 211:                         signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(),
 212:                         validation.code(), validation.explanation());
 213:             }
 214: 
 215:             // Progressive Position Building may add to an existing position only after
 216:             // TP / Profit Lock / SL / validated SELL logic had first priority.
 217:             if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval()) && !profitLock.active()) {
 218:                 WalletManagedPosition managed = walletManagedPositionRepository
 219:                         .findTopBySymbolAndStatusOrderByOpenedAtDesc(symbol, "OPEN")
 220:                         .orElse(null);
 221:                 if (managed != null) {
 222:                     ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision addDecision =
 223:                             executionIntelligenceService.evaluateBuy(
 224:                                     signal,
 225:                                     managed.getAllocatedPositionPercent(),
 226:                                     managed.getEntryStage());
 227:                     if (addDecision.allowed()) {
 228:                         if (walletTradeRepository
 229:                                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(
 230:                                         signal.getId(), "BUY", "EXECUTED")
 231:                                 .isPresent()) {
 232:                             return Optional.of(position);
 233:                         }
 234:                         int addPercent = addDecision.positionPercent();
 235:                         // FIX-056: progressive adds use the same fresh canonical price authority
 236:                         // as initial entries. Signal.latestPrice remains decision-time evidence only.
 237:                         ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh = executionPriceAuthorityService
 238:                                 .resolve(symbol, Instant.now()).orElse(null);
 239:                         if (fresh == null) {
 240:                             log.info("Progressive BUY skipped: no fresh execution price for {}", symbol);
 241:                             return Optional.of(position);
 242:                         }
 243:                         ExecutionIntelligenceService.ExecutionDecision freshDecision =
 244:                                 executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(signal, addDecision, fresh.price());
 245:                         if (!freshDecision.allowed()) return Optional.of(position);
 246:                         addPercent = freshDecision.positionPercent();
 247:                         ExecutionIntelligenceService.EntryQuality entryQuality =
 248:                                 executionIntelligenceService.assessEntryQualityAtPrice(signal, fresh.price());
 249:                         boolean walletAdded = walletAutoExecutionService.executeBuy(
 250:                                 signal, fresh.price(), fresh.observedAt(), addPercent,
 430:         }
 431: 
 432:         return completeClose(position, exitPrice,
 433:                 exitPrice.compareTo(position.getStopLoss()) <= 0 ? PositionStatus.STOPPED : PositionStatus.CLOSED,
 434:                 "MANUAL_CLOSE", "Position was manually closed.", null);
 435:     }
 436: 
 437:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
 438:     public List<PaperPosition> list() {
 439:         return positionRepository.findTop100ByOrderByOpenedAtDesc();
 440:     }
 441: 
 442:     private PaperPosition closeFromSignal(
 443:             PaperPosition position,
 444:             TradeSignal signal,
 445:             PositionStatus status,
 446:             String closeReason,
 447:             String explanation
 448:     ) {
 449:         return completeClose(position, signal.getLatestPrice(), status, closeReason, explanation, signal);
 450:     }
 451: 
 452:     private PaperPosition closeFromProfitLock(
 453:             PaperPosition position,
 454:             TradeSignal signal,
 455:             DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock
 456:     ) {
 457:         BigDecimal exitPrice = signal.getLatestPrice();
 458:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 459:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 460:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 461:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 462:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 463: 
 464:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 465:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 466:         position.setStatus(PositionStatus.CLOSED);
 467:         position.setCloseReason("PROFIT_LOCK");
 468:         position.setExitReason(profitLock.explanation());
 469:         position.setExitSignal(signal);
 470:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 471:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 472: 
 473:         // FIX-020: terminal position close consumes the opportunity that financed this
 474:         // position so old pre-exit evidence cannot immediately open a brand-new trade.
 475:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), signal, "PROFIT_LOCK");
 476: 
 477:         try {
 478:             walletAutoExecutionService.executeProfitLock(signal, exitPrice, profitLock.lockPrice());
 479:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 480:             log.error("Dynamic Profit Lock wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 481:         }
 482:         // FIX-028: profit-lock exits use the same immutable production audit trail.
 483:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, signal, "PROFIT_LOCK", profitLock.explanation());
 484:         return saved;
 485:     }
 486: 
 487:     private PaperPosition completeClose(
 488:             PaperPosition position,
 489:             BigDecimal exitPrice,
 490:             PositionStatus status,
 491:             String closeReason,
 492:             String explanation,
 493:             TradeSignal exitSignal
 494:     ) {
 495:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 496:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 497:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 498:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 499:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);

### src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java
  35: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  36: public class TradeExecutionValidationService {
  37: 
  38:     private static final String EXECUTION_INTERVAL = "1m";
  39:     private static final String CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL = "5m";
  40:     private static final String TREND_INTERVAL = "1h";
  41:     private static final Duration ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(10);
  42:     private static final Duration FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(20);
  43:     private static final Duration ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE = Duration.ofHours(3);
  44: 
  45:     // FIX-11G: this mirrors the existing global CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF in
  46:     // ExecutionIntelligenceService. We deliberately reuse the proven execution-quality
  47:     // boundary instead of inventing a new confidence/quality threshold for this one route.
  48:     // The downstream global Entry Quality guard still remains authoritative and re-checks it.
  49:     private static final int BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY = 50;
  50: 
  51:     private final TradeSignalRepository signalRepository;
  52:     private final WalletSettingsRepository settingsRepository;
  53:     private final EntryConsumptionPolicy entryConsumptionPolicy;
  54:     private final HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService htfAssessment;
  55:     @Autowired(required = false)
  56:     private ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  57:     @Autowired(required = false)
  58:     private BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore htfAuditStore;
  59:     @Autowired(required = false)
  60:     private Fix154Metrics fix154Metrics;
  61: 
  62:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
  63:         ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
  64:         if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
  65: 
  66:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  67:         if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
  68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
  70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
  71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
  72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
  73:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
  74:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
  75:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
  76:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
  77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
  78:             }
  79:         }
  80: 
  81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  88:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  90:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  91:         }
  92: 
  93:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  94:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
  95:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
  96:             audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  97:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  98:         }
  99: 
 100:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
 101:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 102:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 103:             // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
 104:             // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
 105:             // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
 106:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
 107:                     executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
 108:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 109:         };
 110:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 111:         audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 112:         return result;
 113:     }
 114: 
 115:     /**
 335:         int trend = executionSignal.getTrendScore();
 336:         int volume = executionSignal.getVolumeScore();
 337:         int momentum = executionSignal.getMomentumScore();
 338: 
 339:         if (confidence < 85 || trend < 22 || volume < 16 || momentum < 12) {
 340:             return ValidationResult.reject("AGGRESSIVE_PROBE_QUALITY",
 341:                     "Aggressive 25% probe requires confidence>=85, trend>=22, volume>=16, momentum>=12. "
 342:                             + "Actual confidence=" + confidence + ", trend=" + trend + ", volume=" + volume
 343:                             + ", momentum=" + momentum + ".");
 344:         }
 345: 
 346:         return ValidationResult.allow(25, "AGGRESSIVE_PROBE",
 347:                 "Aggressive profile approved a 25% probe: 1m BUY is high quality, 5m is WATCH, and 1h is NEUTRAL.");
 348:     }
 349: 
 350:     private ValidationResult validateBaseSignal(TradeSignal signal, boolean buy) {
 351:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
 352:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution signal is missing required timing data.");
 353:         }
 354:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(signal.getInterval())) {
 355:             return ValidationResult.reject("NON_EXECUTION_TIMEFRAME",
 356:                     "Only 1m signals may trigger normal wallet execution. " + signal.getInterval() + " is context-only.");
 357:         }
 358:         if (buy && !isBullish(signal.getDecision())) {
 359:             return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_BUY", "The 1m decision is not BUY/STRONG_BUY.");
 360:         }
 361:         if (!buy && !isBearish(signal.getDecision())) {
 362:             return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_SELL", "The 1m decision is not SELL/STRONG_SELL.");
 363:         }
 364:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BASE_VALID", "Base execution signal is valid.");
 365:     }
 366: 
 367:     private TradeSignal latestAtOrBefore(TradeSignal signal, String interval) {
 368:         return latestSignal(signal.getSymbol(), interval, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 369:     }
 370: 
 371:     private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> previousSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 372:         if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.previousBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
 373:         return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
 374:     }
 375: 
 376:     private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> latestSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 377:         if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.latestAtOrBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
 378:         return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
 379:     }
 380: 
 381:     private boolean isFresh(TradeSignal signal, Instant reference, Duration maximumAge) {
 382:         return signal != null
 383:                 && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null
 384:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)
 385:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference.minus(maximumAge));
 386:     }
 387: 
 388:     private boolean isBullish(SignalDecision decision) {
 389:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 390:     }
 391: 
 392:     private boolean isBearish(SignalDecision decision) {
 393:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 394:     }
 395: 
 396:     private boolean isWatchOrNeutral(SignalDecision decision) {
 397:         return decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
 398:     }
 399: 
 400:     private WalletSettings settings() {
 401:         return settingsRepository.findById(1L)
 402:                 .orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
 403:                         .id(1L)
 404:                         .executionProfile(ExecutionProfile.BALANCED.name())
 405:                         .requireNewBuyTransition(true)
 406:                         .dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true)
 407:                         .profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))
 408:                         .profitLockInitialPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(40))
 409:                         .profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10))
 410:                         .build());
 411:     }
 412: 
 413:     private ValidationResult cap(ValidationResult result, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment) {
 414:         if (!result.allowed() || assessment.maxInitialPercent()==null) return result;
 415:         int capped=Math.min(result.positionPercent(),assessment.maxInitialPercent());
 416:         if (capped != result.positionPercent() && fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softCappedEntry();
 417:         return capped==result.positionPercent()?result:ValidationResult.allow(capped,result.code(),result.explanation()+" FIX-154 soft-HTF cap="+capped+"%.");
 418:     }
 419: 
 420:     /** Used only after progressive raw-HTF checks have already failed; never changes an add decision. */
 421:     public void recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 422:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 423:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 424:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 425:         if (!isFresh(five, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)
 426:                 || !isFresh(one, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) return;
 427:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment = htfAssessment.assess(five, true, one, true);
 428:         if (assessment.band() == HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Band.SOFT_CONFLICT) {
 429:             if (fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softAddBlocked();
 430:             audit(referenceSignal, "PROGRESSIVE_ADD", assessment, "SOFT_HTF_ADD_BLOCKED",

 567:         // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
 568:         // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
 569:         // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.
 570:         ReplayFreshSignalProfiler profiler = new ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(runId, dataSource);
 571:         // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
 572:         // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
 573:         analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 574:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
 575:         // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
 576:         // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
 577:         // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
 578:         // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
 579:         // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
 580:         for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
 581:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 582:             ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);
 583:             Candle candle = replay.candle();
 584:             Instant evaluationTime = candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(replay.interval()));
 585:             boolean inRequestedWindow = !evaluationTime.isBefore(executionStart) && !evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd);
 586:             try {
 587:                 long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 588:                 java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
 589:                 try {
 590:                     snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 591:                             ? technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(

 593:                             : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 594:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
 595:                 } finally {
 596:                     profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 597:                 }
 598:                 if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
 599:                     if (inRequestedWindow) {
 600:                         errors++;
 601:                         saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
 602:                                 "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
 603:                     }
 604:                     continue;
 605:                 }
 606: 
 607:                 // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
 608:                 // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
 609:                 // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.
 610:                 if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;
 611: 
 612:                 replayScope.reference(evaluationTime);
 613:                 operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 614:                 TradeSignal generated;
 615:                 try {
 616:                     generated = analysisService.analyzeForRegression(snapshot.get(), evaluationTime);
 617:                 } finally {
 618:                     profiler.recordAnalysis(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 619:                 }
 620:                 replayScope.appendSignal(generated);
 621:                 SignalDecision decision = generated.getDecision();
 622:                 if (replay.primary()) generatedSignals.add(generated);
 623: 
 624:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 625:                     if ("1m".equals(replay.interval())) { oneMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneMinuteBuys++; }
 780:             case "1m" -> 60L;
 781:             case "5m" -> 300L;
 782:             case "1h" -> 3600L;
 783:             default -> 60L;
 784:         };
 785:     }
 786: 
 787:     private int intervalOrder(String interval) {
 788:         return switch (interval) {
 789:             case "1h" -> 0;
 790:             case "5m" -> 1;
 791:             default -> 2;
 792:         };
 793:     }
 794: 
 795:     private boolean isBuy(SignalDecision decision) {
 796:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 797:     }

 442:     private PaperPosition closeFromSignal(
 443:             PaperPosition position,
 444:             TradeSignal signal,
 445:             PositionStatus status,
 446:             String closeReason,
 447:             String explanation
 448:     ) {
 449:         return completeClose(position, signal.getLatestPrice(), status, closeReason, explanation, signal);
 450:     }
 451: 
 452:     private PaperPosition closeFromProfitLock(
 453:             PaperPosition position,
 454:             TradeSignal signal,
 455:             DynamicProfitLockService.Evaluation profitLock
 456:     ) {
 457:         BigDecimal exitPrice = signal.getLatestPrice();
 458:         BigDecimal remainingPnl = exitPrice.subtract(position.getEntryPrice(), MC)
 459:                 .multiply(position.getQuantity(), MC);
 460:         // FIX-11T: preserve any P&L already realized by a Near-TP partial harvest.
 461:         BigDecimal pnl = (position.getRealizedPnl() == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : position.getRealizedPnl())
 462:                 .add(remainingPnl, MC);
 463: 
 464:         position.setExitPrice(exitPrice);
 465:         position.setRealizedPnl(pnl);
 466:         position.setStatus(PositionStatus.CLOSED);
 467:         position.setCloseReason("PROFIT_LOCK");
 468:         position.setExitReason(profitLock.explanation());
 469:         position.setExitSignal(signal);
 470:         position.setClosedAt(Instant.now());
 471:         PaperPosition saved = positionRepository.save(position);
 472: 
 473:         // FIX-020: terminal position close consumes the opportunity that financed this
 474:         // position so old pre-exit evidence cannot immediately open a brand-new trade.
 475:         executionIntelligenceService.completePositionOpportunity(position.getSymbol(), signal, "PROFIT_LOCK");
 476: 
 477:         try {
 478:             walletAutoExecutionService.executeProfitLock(signal, exitPrice, profitLock.lockPrice());
 479:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
 480:             log.error("Dynamic Profit Lock wallet exit failed for signal {}: {}", signal.getId(), ex.getMessage(), ex);
 481:         }
 482:         // FIX-028: profit-lock exits use the same immutable production audit trail.
 483:         productionExitAuditService.record(saved, signal, "PROFIT_LOCK", profitLock.explanation());
 484:         return saved;
 485:     }

### src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/OneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.java
